* **命题 `prop:front-brst-bilocal` — 双局域 PDF 算符 BRST 分类的矩约化** [A] (planning tag at the merge: the source grade; **upgrade to [R, R_PT] only if N04 passes**, incl. the circularity check below).
  - *Statement.* Let $O_q(w^-)=\bar\psi(w)\gamma^+W[w,0]\psi(0)$, $w=(0^+,w^-,\mathbf 0_T)$, and let $O_g(w^-)$ be the analogous gluon operator (`def:pdf-lightray`). In physical (on-shell, BRST-invariant) matrix elements, to every order of renormalized perturbation theory, $O^R=Z_{OO}\otimes O_{(0)}$ with
    $$\int_0^1dz\,z^{N-1}Z_{OO}(z)=Z^{(N)}_{\rm twist\text{-}2}\quad\text{for all }N,$$
    the admixtures of BRST-exact (class A) and equation-of-motion (class B) operators drop out, and $Z_{OO}$ is uniquely fixed by its moments (compact support; for the $C$-even/$C$-odd combinations $q\pm\bar q$ and for the gluon only even or odd $N$ occur, and uniqueness follows from `thm:appK-muntz`). Hence the triangular mixing (`Collins2011` eq. (11.20); VF-FRO-15)
    $$\begin{pmatrix}O\\A\\B\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}Z_{OO}&Z_{OA}&Z_{OB}\\0&Z_{AA}&Z_{AB}\\0&0&Z_{BB}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}O_{(0)}\\A_{(0)}\\B_{(0)}\end{pmatrix}$$
    restricted to physical matrix elements holds for the bilocal PDF operators. `% VF-FRO-15`
  - *Inputs in this book* (they replace the hypotheses of the frontier report, whose numbering is never printed): local classification for $\bar\psi\gamma^+(iD^+)^{N-1}\psi$ and $F^{+\mu}(iD^+)^{N-2}F^+{}_\mu$ — all indices "+", hence symmetric and traceless, exactly the twist-2 operators (`prop:appJ-local-operators`); support of the forward matrix elements in $[-1,1]$ order by order (`thm:pdf-support-pt`); the bare non-local operator is the generating function of the bare local ones (`prop:appL-bare`; exact for compact support, since the Fourier transform of a compactly supported distribution is entire with Taylor coefficients = moments) and the renormalized moment relation (`prop:appL-renormalized`).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及算符与部分子态。）
  - `\uses{def:pdf-lightray, prop:appJ-local-operators, thm:pdf-support-pt, prop:appL-bare, prop:appL-renormalized, thm:appK-muntz}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (0.4 pp): (1) moments of the bare bilocal operator = bare local twist-2 operators (`prop:appL-bare`); (2) local classification ⇒ in physical matrix elements only $Z^{(N)}_{\rm twist\text{-}2}$ survives (`prop:appJ-local-operators`); (3) compact support ⇒ the matrix element of the bilocal operator is determined by its moments (`thm:pdf-support-pt`); (4) define $Z_{OO}$ through its moments and prove uniqueness (`thm:appK-muntz`); (5) the renormalized moment relation closes the argument (`prop:appL-renormalized`). One-loop instance verified graph by graph (`research/c_pdf/DERIVATION.md` §5.4).
  - *Status and gap (地位与缺口).* [A] at the merge (integrator decision MD7, "frontier grades conservative"): the frontier report grades the reduction [A] (its support hypothesis is only [A] there); in this book all inputs are [R] or mathematics, and claim N04 is confirmed (two confirmations) **as an [A] proposition with prior art**: the general off-forward version is `Braun:2019qtp` eqs. (2.4), (2.6), (3.1), (3.2) (the idea goes back to Balitsky–Braun 1989), so the statement stays [A], is printed without novelty word ("not this work") and is not promoted to [R, R_PT] (the circularity check of the App L proof of `prop:appL-renormalized` was not part of the verification). Caveat: for forward on-shell partonic matrix elements in dimensional regularization, BRST and EOM operators need not drop out. The weakest step: this circularity question (E-S not applicable; an algebraic reduction). What it does **not** cover: the classification step *inside* the factorization proof (multilocal operators with fields at different light-cone positions before gauge invariance is imposed; `conj:front-brst-multilocal`); TMD operators (staples, rapidity divergences); off-shell renormalization of the light-ray operators themselves (not needed for physical predictions).
* **猜想 `conj:front-brst-multilocal` — 多局域光线算符的 BRST 分类** [C].
  - *Statement.* Every BRST-invariant multilocal operator of ghost number zero, supported on a light-like line, of the type that appears in the leading-region decomposition of the off-shell Green function (`Collins2011` (11.12)), is the sum of a gauge-invariant operator, a BRST variation (class A) and an operator that vanishes by the equations of motion (class B).
  - `\uses{def:prelim-brst, prop:appJ-local-operators}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]: assumed by Collins (quote in `rem:front-k-gluon`); wording of the literature status only as 「据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索（INSPIRE 与网络检索），尚未见非定域（多局域）BRST 分类的证明」 (A06 item (9): holds). Natural tool: localization by an auxiliary field (the Wilson line as a field), as in the renormalizability proofs for space-like Wilson-line operators (`Ji:2017oey`, `Ishikawa:2017faj`, `Green:2017xeu`; Ch22 `prop:lattice-renormalizability`, `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`, pointers). Over-claim to avoid: 「Collins (2011) 证明了非定域算符的 BRST 分类」. Coordination: App J `rem:appJ-nonlocal` ([A] in v1) should point here and to `prop:front-brst-bilocal` (§F5).
* *Budget of §24.10.* 1.2 pp.

#### §24.11 `sec:front-smallx`

* Framing (text, 0.1 pp): small-$x$ "factorization" is a different expansion from the collinear one — the Regge limit $s\gg|t|$ (DIS: $x\to0$ at fixed $Q^2$), organized by rapidity factorization of Wilson lines (shockwave picture): projectile impact factors ⊗ target Wilson-line correlators evolved in rapidity (BK/JIMWLK). Its results are all-order statements within the eikonal (Regge) expansion at fixed logarithmic accuracy, not leading-power theorems with power-counting control; the grades below are relative to that framework. The LO BK equation (VF-FRO-12 = VF-MISC-4) is stated in Ch17 (`thm:smallx-bk`), not here.
* **命题 `prop:front-nlo-bk` — NLO BK/JIMWLK 与三圈 BK 核** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在 eikonal（Regge）展开的次领头对数精度上，」 (a) the NLO evolution of colour dipoles contains quadratic and cubic non-linearities; the NLO kernel is the sum of a running-coupling part ($\propto\beta_0$), a conformal part ($1\to3$ dipole transitions) and a non-conformal double-logarithmic term; its forward linearization agrees with the NLO BFKL kernel (`Balitsky:2007feb`; the $\alpha_s^2\zeta(3)\times$(dipole) mismatch reported in its §VIII was traced by the same authors to an erroneous evaluation of an integral assumed to vanish and disappears after correction, `Balitsky:2009xg` §6, "In QCD"; quark part and running coupling `Balitsky:2006wa`, `Kovchegov:2006vj`); (b) NLO JIMWLK (`Balitsky:2013fea`, `Kovner:2013ona`; conformal symmetry `Kovner:2014xia`; "unabridged" `Kovner:2014lca`; abstracts); (c) the three-loop BK kernel in planar $\mathcal N=4$ SYM via the equivalence with non-global logarithms, with a Lorentz-covariant IR/collinear subtraction method and checks against the Pomeron trajectory and the twist-2 spectrum (`Caron-Huot:2016tzz`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题是关于 Wilson 线算符的演化方程。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bk, thm:smallx-bfkl}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] within the eikonal framework; no disagreement with NLO BFKL is printed: the $\zeta(3)$ term of `Balitsky:2007feb` §VIII was withdrawn by its authors (`Balitsky:2009xg` §6, "In QCD"; claim E34 item (iii), refuted as a disagreement, Q3) — never attribute it to longitudinal cutoffs.
* **命题 `prop:front-bk-collinear` — NLO BK 的不稳定性与共线重求和** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「对现实的初始条件，」 the NLO BK solution is unstable (`Lappi:2015fma`); kinematic constraints and collinear resummation cure it (`Beuf:2014uia`, `Iancu:2015vea`) (abstracts).
  - `\uses{prop:front-nlo-bk}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: the resummation prescriptions differ beyond the controlled logarithms; a scheme-independent collinear improvement is open (`rem:front-open`).
* **命题 `prop:front-regge` — 高能极限中的 Wilson 线方法、胶子 Regge 化与 Regge 极限的红外奇异部分** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) The eikonal approximation with rapidity-dependent collections of Wilson lines (Balitsky–JIMWLK) reproduces Reggeization and BFKL; from four loops BFKL dynamics is incompatible with the dipole formula for IR divergences; in the strict planar limit the theory can be formulated with a finite, process-dependent number of Wilson lines without reference to a reggeized gluon, checked exactly in $\mathcal N=4$ (`Caron-Huot:2013fea`, abstract, §4 checked). (b) The IR-singular part of $2\to2$ amplitudes in the high-energy limit is given in closed form to all orders at NLL (two-Reggeon contribution), predicting the Regge limit of the soft anomalous dimension at any loop order (`Caron-Huot:2017zfo`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子振幅。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bfkl, thm:sudakov-ir-poles}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: all-order arguments within the eikonal (Regge) expansion at fixed logarithmic accuracy (Wilson-line method; (a) checked exactly only in planar $\mathcal N=4$; (b) all orders at NLL); sources partly at abstract level 「据该文摘要」; not a leading-power theorem of QCD (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.9, BK/JIMWLK item).
* **命题 `prop:front-regge-pole` — 简单 Regge 极点因子化在 NNLL 失效** [V].
  - *Statement.* Using IR factorization, the simplest Regge-pole factorization of $2\to2$ amplitudes fails at NNLL in $\ln(s/|t|)$: a two-loop non-logarithmic double pole and non-factorizing single-logarithmic singular terms at three loops; the hard functions vanish in $d=4$ to all orders up to NLL (`DelDuca:2013ara`, `DelDuca:2014cya`, 「据该文摘要」).
  - `\uses{def:smallx-regge, thm:sudakov-ir-poles}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [V] for Regge-pole factorization beyond NLL, evidence: explicit calculation based on IR factorization ([A], `thm:sudakov-ir-poles`); the corrected multi-Reggeon picture is [A] (`prop:front-regge`(b)).
* **命题 `prop:front-smallx-tmd` — 小 $x$ 的 TMD 与 Sudakov 对数** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在色玻璃凝聚框架的领头阶与领头对数精度上，」 (a) effective $k_t$-factorization for dijets in dilute–dense collisions; the Weizsäcker–Williams and dipole gluon distributions are the universal small-$x$ TMDs, with process-dependent gauge links at small $x$ (`Dominguez:2011wm`); (b) Sudakov double logarithms combined with small-$x$ resummation in the saturation formalism (`Mueller:2012uf`) (abstracts). (c)（部分结果，\tagP）rapidity evolution of gluon TMDs from low to moderate $x$ (`Balitsky:2015qba`); gauge-invariant TMD factorization at small $x$, rapidity-only factorization, Sudakov–BFKL interpolation (`Balitsky:2020jzt`, `Balitsky:2021fer`, `Balitsky:2023hmh`, `Balitsky:2026lop`) (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （高能展开框架内的陈述；靶的非微扰信息作为初始条件输入。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bk}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (LO/LL) for (a), (b); (c) [P].
* **命题 `prop:front-smallx-helicity` — 小 $x$ 螺旋度演化：截距与 BER 的分歧** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在双对数近似与大 $N_c$（或大 $N_c\&N_f$）下（据该文摘要），」 helicity evolution at small $x$ (double logarithms $\alpha_s\ln^2(1/x)$) follows from polarized dipole amplitudes, i.e. sub-eikonal Wilson-line operators (`Kovchegov:2015pbl`, erratum; `Kovchegov:2016weo`), in the revised form of `Cougoulic:2022gbk`; its exact large-$N_c$ solution gives $\Delta\Sigma\sim\Delta G\sim g_1\sim(1/x)^{\alpha_h}$ with
    $$\alpha_h\simeq3.66074\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_sN_c}{2\pi}}\quad(\texttt{Borden:2023ugd}),\qquad\alpha_h^{\rm BER}\simeq3.66394\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_sN_c}{2\pi}}\quad(\text{infrared evolution equations, }\texttt{Bartels:1996wc});$$
    the resummed $\Delta\gamma_{GG}(\omega)$ agrees with the fixed-order anomalous dimensions through three loops and differs from the Bartels–Ermolaev–Ryskin result (`Bartels:1995iu`, `Bartels:1996wc`) from four loops on; large $N_c\&N_f$: `Adamiak:2023okq`, `Borden:2024bxa` (quark↔gluon transition operators), `Borden:2025ehe` (analytic solution; a slight disagreement with BER and with Blümlein–Vogt `Blumlein:1996hb` persists). `% VF-FRO-17` (in the book the formula is typeset with the keys as `\cite`, not `\texttt`)
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （高能展开框架内的陈述。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bk, def:pdf-polarized}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (double-logarithmic approximation, large $N_c$ or large $N_c\&N_f$); the disagreement with BER is an open problem, printed without a verdict (E34(iv), Q3; 2026 status `Borden:2025ehe`). Related (abstracts): gluon orbital angular momentum at small $x$ (`Hatta:2016aoc`), small-$x$ evolution of the gluon GPD $E_g$ (`Hatta:2022bxn`) — [A]/[P] in words.
* *Budget of §24.11.* 1.2 pp.

#### §24.12 `sec:front-dps`

* Text (0.1 pp): two hard scatterings in one $pp$ collision (e.g. double Drell–Yan); formally power suppressed for integrated cross sections but leading for transverse-momentum-differential ones ($q_{T,i}\ll Q_i$) and enhanced for many final states at small $x$. Framework: colour-correlated DPDs, power counting, elements of an all-order proof, links to GPDs and the lattice (`DiehlOstermeierSchafer2012`, erratum); colour correlations, interference, evolution, the $1\to2$ splitting singularity ($\propto1/y^2$ at small separation $y$) and the double-counting problem (`Manohar:2012jr`, `Manohar:2012pe`) (abstracts). Glauber cancellation for double DY to all orders, integrated and $q_T$-differential (`Diehl:2015bca`), and soft-gluon decoupling into Wilson-line vacuum expectation values with non-abelian Ward identities (`Diehl:2018wfy`) are part of the core statement `thm:break-double-dy` (Ch18, [A], pointer; the "DGOPS: Glauber cancellation established only for DY" item of A06 is worded there).
* **命题 `prop:front-dps-scheme` — 无重复计数的双部分子散射** [A].
  - *Statement.*
    $$\sigma=\sigma_{\rm SPS}+\sigma_{\rm DPS}-\sigma_{\rm sub},\qquad\frac{d\sigma_{\rm DPS}}{dx_1dx_2d\bar x_1d\bar x_2}=\sum\hat\sigma_{a_1b_1}\hat\sigma_{a_2b_2}\int d^2y\,\Phi^2(y\nu)\,F_{b_1b_2}(\bar x_i,y)\,F_{a_1a_2}(x_i,y),$$
    with a cut-off function $\Phi(u)\to1$ for $u\gg1$, $\Phi(u)=O(u^\delta)$ at small $u$ (e.g. $\Theta(u-b_0)$ or $1-e^{-u^2/4}$), $\nu\sim Q$; $\sigma_{\rm sub}$ is the DPS formula with both DPDs replaced by their fixed-order perturbative splitting forms (for 1v1: $\sigma_{1v1,{\rm pt}}$). At small $y$ the $\Phi$ dependence cancels between $\sigma_{\rm DPS}$ and $\sigma_{\rm sub}$ (leaving SPS); at large $y$, $\sigma_{\rm SPS}\simeq\sigma_{\rm sub}$ (leaving DPS). DPDs are thereby defined as operator matrix elements usable at higher orders (`Diehl:2017kgu` §4.1, read). `% VF-FRO-13`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：\cref{asm:intro-composite}、\cref{asm:intro-bridge}（MA0、MA1+MA2）。
  - `\uses{def:break-dpdf, thm:break-double-dy}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: scheme consistency argued at fixed order; the cancellation holds only up to higher orders when the DPDs are evolved. Full DPS factorization with coloured final states or jets stays [C] (`conj:break-dps-coloured`, Ch18, pointer).
* **命题 `prop:front-rapidity-renorm` — 多部分子软因子快度发散的重整化定理** [A].
  - *Statement.* Rapidity divergences of multi-parton soft factors arise from exchanges with the distant transverse plane and are structurally equivalent to UV divergences via a conformal map; hence a renormalization theorem for rapidity divergences (proof via the conformal transformation), a definition of multi-parton TMDs, and an exact relation between soft and rapidity anomalous dimensions (the three-loop rapidity anomalous dimension is derived) (`Vladimirov:2017ksc`, 「据该文摘要」).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及软因子（Wilson 线真空期望值）。）
  - `\uses{def:tmd-soft, def:break-dpdf}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (abstract level). Forward pointers from Ch22 and Ch23 (`prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear` status) land here; Vladimirov calls the rapidity factorization of the SIDIS soft factor "the open question" (`Vladimirov:2017ksc`, JHEP version, p. 32; not in arXiv v2; as quoted in `Liu:2023pjb`).
* **命题 `prop:front-dpd-evolution` — 色关联 DPD 的演化与匹配** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「到次领头阶（两圈核），」 TMD DPS factorization, evolution and matching (`Buffing:2017mqm`, erratum/addendum 2021); evolution kernels for colour-non-singlet DPDs at NLO (`Diehl:2022rxb`); two-loop $1\to2$ splitting (`Diehl:2019rdh`); quark-mass effects in the splitting (`Diehl:2022dia`); momentum and number sum rules in position space (`Diehl:2020xyg`); review `Diehl:2017wew` (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及演化核与部分子层次的匹配系数。）
  - `\uses{def:break-dpdf, prop:front-rapidity-renorm}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] (computed to NLO); phenomenology of colour-correlated DPDs is open.
* *Budget of §24.12.* 0.8 pp. Optional figure `fig:front-dps-double-counting`.

#### §24.13 `sec:front-jets`

* Text: the $e^+e^-$ factorization theorem for jet processes with non-global logarithms (`Becher:2015hka`, `Becher:2016mmh`; VF-FRO-14 — hard functions $\mathcal H_m$ × multi-Wilson-line soft functions $\mathcal S_m$, RGEs as matrices in multiplicity space, BMS at LL and large $N_c$; coft modes for narrow cones) is the core statement `thm:jets-ngl-factorization` (Ch16, [A]); the light-jet mass and hemisphere soft function (`Becher:2016omr`, abstract) likewise. This section records what goes beyond it.
* **命题 `prop:front-ngl` — 非全局对数：超出领头对数与测量驱动的因子化** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) A colour density matrix resums non-global logarithms to all logarithmic orders; its anomalous dimension is known at LO and NLO; by a conformal transformation it is equivalent to shockwave (Regge) evolution, rederiving NLO BFKL/Balitsky–JIMWLK, and the exponentiation of divergences holds to all logarithmic orders (`Caron-Huot:2015bja`, abstract). (b) 「在大 $N_c$ 与次领头对数精度上，」 NLL resummation of NGLs: integro-differential equations in planar large-$N_c$ QCD in dimensional regularization and in a finite four-dimensional form, checked against full QCD at $O(\alpha_s^2)$ (`Banfi:2021owj`); jet calculus (`Banfi:2021xzn`); two-loop anomalous dimension (`Becher:2021urs`); NLL resummation at the LHC in a large-$N_c$ parton shower (`Becher:2023vrh`); 2026 preprints on the two-loop anomalous dimension for non-global and clustering logarithms (`Becher:2026zon`) and NLL NGLs in Higgs production (`Becher:2026iim`) (abstracts). (c) NGLs factorize and are resummed down to an unresolved scale by making sufficiently many measurements ("dressed gluons"; `Larkoski:2015zka`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子截面。）
  - `\uses{thm:jets-ngl-factorization, thm:jets-bms}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] ((b): large $N_c$, NLL; abstract level). Coordination: Ch16 `prop:jets-ngl-beyond` ([P]) concerns finite-$N_c$ and fixed-order results; the [A] here is restricted to large $N_c$ (§F5). At hadron colliders Glauber phases produce SLLs (§24.6).
* **命题 `prop:front-jet-substructure` — 喷注子结构、修饰喷注与半单举喷注函数** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) 「在 soft-drop 幂次计数 $z_{\rm cut}\ll1$ 中，」 a large class of soft-drop groomed observables (incl. jet mass) factorizes; without the groomed soft wide-angle radiation all singular contributions are collinear, so the normalized distributions have no NGLs to all orders and are process independent up to the quark/gluon fraction; NNLL resummation (`Frye:2016aiz`); (b) hadronization in groomed jets: in an intermediate jet-mass region the leading power corrections are three universal non-perturbative parameters with perturbative coefficients; at smaller mass a non-normalized shape function (`Hoang:2019ceu`); (c) semi-inclusive jet functions $J_i(z,\omega_J,\mu)$ obey exactly time-like DGLAP evolution (`prop:pdf-timelike-dglap`; $\ln R$ resummation), and single-inclusive jet production shares the hard functions of single-inclusive hadron production with $D_i^h\to J_i$ (`Kang:2016mcy`) (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子截面；(b) 的非微扰参数作为输入。）
  - `\uses{def:jets-algorithms, prop:pdf-timelike-dglap}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] ((c): NLO + RG consistency; abstract level).
* **命题 `prop:front-lightray-ope` — 能量关联子的共线因子化与共形场论中的光线 OPE** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) The energy–energy correlator factorizes at leading power in the collinear limit in a generic QFT, with anomalous dimensions given by moments of time-like splitting functions (`prop:pdf-timelike-dglap`); NNLL in QCD (`Dixon:2019uzg`, abstract). (b) 「在共形场论中（非微扰），」 null-integrated (light-ray) operators on the same null plane have a convergent OPE ("light-ray OPE"; celestial blocks for event shapes), derived within non-perturbative CFT using the Lorentzian inversion formula (`Kologlu:2019mfz`, abstract) — the non-perturbative analogue of the collinear factorization of event shapes, not a QCD statement.
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （(a) 是微扰陈述；(b) 是共形场论中的非微扰陈述。）
  - `\uses{def:jets-algorithms, prop:pdf-timelike-dglap}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] ((b): CFT, non-perturbative; the only non-perturbative positive statement of this chapter).
* *Budget of §24.13.* 1.0 pp.

#### §24.14 `sec:front-quarkonium`

* Text: NRQCD factorization (`BodwinBraatenLepage1995`) $d\sigma(H)=\sum_nd\hat\sigma_n(\Lambda)\langle\mathcal O^H_n(\Lambda)\rangle$ is [A] for decays (`thm:heavy-nrqcd-decay`, Ch20) and a conjecture for production (`conj:heavy-nrqcd-production`, Ch20, [C]); the questions are (i) whether the IR divergences of the short-distance coefficients match those of the LDMEs to all orders, (ii) universality of the LDMEs.
* **命题 `prop:front-nrqcd-ir` — NNLO 的红外极点与规范完备的八重态算符** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在 NNLO（两圈）阶，」 (a) in gluon fragmentation at large $p_T$ the NNLO IR poles are not matched by the conventional octet production operators; gauge invariance and factorization require operators with Wilson lines (non-abelian phases); with these "gauge-completed" matrix elements factorization is restored at NNLO, first to $O(v^2)$ and then to all orders in $v$ for the octet→singlet transition (`NayakQiuSterman2005a`, `NayakQiuSterman2005b`, `NayakQiuSterman2006`, abstracts); caveat (NQS 2005): beyond NNLO, "in the absence of special cancellations", further non-local operators outside the NRQCD velocity expansion may be needed. (b) A covariant two-loop calculation of the LDME for an S-wave colour-octet pair fragmenting into a colour-singlet pair of arbitrary orbital angular momentum finds the IR divergences independent of the Wilson-line direction, as universality requires; the results differ from NQS in some respects (sources identified); both are consistent with NRQCD factorization; "the general principle that underlies this confirmation … has yet to be revealed" (`Bodwin:2019bpf`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子层次的匹配。）
  - `\uses{def:heavy-nrqcd}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] (two loops) (A25). Over-claim to avoid (S58): 「NQS 的规范完备 Wilson 线在 NNLO 不改变结果」 — they are necessary at NNLO.
* **注 `rem:front-quarkonium-alternatives` — 唯象张力与替代方案** (no tag). The LP + NLP collinear factorization in $1/p_T$ (single-parton and heavy-quark-pair fragmentation; `Kang:2011mg`, `Kang:2014tta`, `Kang:2014pya`, `Fleming:2012wy`, `Ma:2013yla`, `Ma:2014eja`, `Bodwin:2014gia`) is the core statement `prop:heavy-quarkonium-fragmentation` (Ch20, [A]); it rests on single-inclusive collinear factorization at high $p_T$, for which `Collins2011` p. 571 singles out the NQS 2005 argument as the "notable exception" to the lack of detailed proofs (A13: check page). LHCb $\eta_c$ data vs heavy-quark spin symmetry: "challenges NRQCD factorization" (`Butenschoen:2014dra`) vs consistent fits (`Han:2014jya`, `Zhang:2014ybe`) — both sides reported (A25); the polarization puzzle persists (not reviewed). Soft-gluon factorization (`Ma:2017xno`, abstract): soft hadronization effects treated without expanding first in the relative velocity, claimed better velocity convergence — a proposal with partial checks (a conjecture as a theorem, in words).
* *Budget of §24.14.* 0.7 pp.

#### §24.15 `sec:front-qed`

* **定理 `thm:front-luxqed` — 由结构函数确定质子的光子 PDF（LUXqed）** [A].
  - *Statement.* In the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme
    $$xf_{\gamma/p}(x,\mu^2)=\frac1{2\pi\alpha(\mu^2)}\int_x^1\frac{dz}z\Big\{\int_{\frac{x^2m_p^2}{1-z}}^{\frac{\mu^2}{1-z}}\frac{dQ^2}{Q^2}\alpha^2(Q^2)\Big[\Big(zp_{\gamma q}(z)+\frac{2x^2m_p^2}{Q^2}\Big)F_2\Big(\frac xz,Q^2\Big)-z^2F_L\Big(\frac xz,Q^2\Big)\Big]-\alpha^2(\mu^2)z^2F_2\Big(\frac xz,\mu^2\Big)\Big\},$$
    $zp_{\gamma q}(z)=1+(1-z)^2$, including all terms of order $\alpha L(\alpha_sL)^n$, $\alpha(\alpha_sL)^n$ and $\alpha^2L^2(\alpha_sL)^n$ ($L=\ln\mu^2/m_p^2$); $F_{2,L}$ include the elastic parts $F_2^{\rm el}=\frac{G_E^2+\tau G_M^2}{1+\tau}\delta(1-x)$, $F_L^{\rm el}=\frac{G_E^2}\tau\delta(1-x)$, $\tau=Q^2/4m_p^2$ (`Manohar:2016nzj` eq. (6), read). `% VF-FRO-11`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：\cref{asm:intro-wightman}、\cref{asm:intro-composite}、\cref{asm:intro-bridge}（MA0、MA1+MA2）。
  - `\uses{def:pdf-gluon, thm:disall-factorization}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: a fictitious BSM process $l+p\to L+X$ through a transition magnetic moment $(e/\Lambda)\bar L\sigma^{\mu\nu}F_{\mu\nu}l$; its cross section is computed (i) exactly in terms of $F_2$, $F_L$ (the lepton current is not renormalized) and (ii) by collinear factorization with $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDFs (the photon PDF defined as the QED analogue of `def:pdf-gluon`); equating the two to the stated accuracy fixes $f_\gamma$. The follow-up (`Manohar:2017eqh`, abstract) derives the result from the operator definition, gives an exact representation in terms of $F_2$, $F_L$ to all orders in QED and QCD including non-perturbative corrections, the formula one order higher, polarized and TMD photon PDFs, and $P_{\gamma i}$ at $O(\alpha\alpha_s)$ and $O(\alpha^2)$ in agreement with direct calculations.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: relies on collinear factorization of the auxiliary process (`thm:disall-factorization`, [A]); the operator-definition derivation makes it process independent. Coordination: Ch11 (photon structure) uses VF-FRO-11 as well (§F5).
* **例 `ex:front-luxqed-pointlike` — 点状靶极限** (no tag). For a point-like Dirac fermion target (elastic only, $G_E=G_M=1$: $F_2=\delta(1-x')$, $F_L=\frac{4m^2}{Q^2}\delta(1-x')$) the formula gives
  $$xf_\gamma=\frac\alpha{2\pi}\big[1+(1-x)^2\big]\Big[\ln\frac{\mu^2}{m^2}-2\ln x-1\Big]+\frac{\alpha m^2x^2(1-x)}{\pi\mu^2},$$
  i.e. the $O(\alpha)$ $\overline{\rm MS}$ photon-in-lepton PDF of `Frixione:2019lga` (eq. (4.189), read) up to a power correction; dropping $F_L$ would shift the constant by $\frac\alpha{2\pi}4(1-x)$, dropping the $\overline{\rm MS}$ conversion term by $\frac\alpha{2\pi}x^2$ (OTHERS `s3`; N28). `\uses{thm:front-luxqed}`. 0.25 pp.
* **命题 `prop:front-lepton-pdf` — 轻子与电子的 PDF、QED 与混合演化核** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) Lepton PDFs of the proton from the same structure-function input at NLO (`Buonocore:2020nai`); (b) electron, positron and photon PDFs of the electron at NLL in QED with all-order large-$z$ resummation matched to $O(\alpha^3)$ (initial conditions `Frixione:2019lga`; `Bertone:2019hks`, erratum); (c) QED and mixed QCD×QED corrections to the Altarelli–Parisi kernels at $O(\alpha\alpha_s)$ (`deFlorian:2015ujt`) and $O(\alpha^2)$ by "abelianization" (`deFlorian:2016gvk`) — explicit kernels at the stated orders (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：\cref{asm:intro-bridge}（MA1+MA2）（(a)；(b)、(c) 只涉及部分子层次的演化）。
  - `\uses{thm:front-luxqed, thm:pdf-dglap}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (abstract level); (c) are finite-order results (in words).
* **命题 `prop:front-ew-pdf` — 电弱 PDF 与 $Q\gg M_W$ 的电弱对数** [A].
  - *Statement.* At $Q\gg M_W$ electroweak PDFs and their evolution resum EW logarithms (`Bauer:2017isx`; polarization effects `Bauer:2018arx`; $W$, $Z$ PDFs from structure functions `Fornal:2018znf`); in an NLL framework the objects of the factorization theorem need not be $SU(2)\times U(1)$ singlets, and DGLAP-type evolution captures all EW logarithms only for sufficiently inclusive measurements (e.g. invariant mass), while identifying a final-state lepton requires fragmentation functions and soft exchanges (`Manohar:2018kfx`) (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：\cref{asm:intro-bridge}（MA1+MA2）。
  - `\uses{thm:pdf-dglap}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (abstract level).
* **命题 `prop:front-ew-violation` — 电弱 Bloch–Nordsieck 抵消的失效与测量弱荷的观测量** [V].
  - *Statement.* (a) Collider initial states carry definite non-abelian (weak-isospin) charges, so EW Sudakov double logarithms do not cancel in inclusive TeV-scale cross sections: the Bloch–Nordsieck cancellation fails for non-singlet initial states (`Ciafaloni:2000df`, 「据该文摘要」). (b) For observables that measure the weak charge of final states in hadronic collisions, non-factorizable EW corrections break the parton-model picture at leading power, starting at $O(\alpha_s^2\alpha_W^2\log^4(Q^2/M_W^2))$, not resummable by DGLAP alone (`Baumgart:2018ntv`, 「据该文摘要」).
  - `\uses{def:ir-safe}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [V], evidence: explicit perturbative calculations (as computed in those papers; A25). The QCD analogue is `prop:ir-bn-failure` (Ch4, [V], pointer). Open: resummation of the non-factorizing EW logarithms.
* *Budget of §24.15.* 1.0 pp.

#### §24.16 `sec:front-status`

* **Table `tab:front-status`.** Columns: label | statement (one line) | tag (+ qualifier) | weakest step / bottleneck (`asm:` labels) | error type of that step (§4.7; "—" for statements about single integrals, models or explicit calculations) | refereed? (「预印本」 where applicable). One row per tagged statement of this chapter (55 rows: 7 [V], 26 [A], 2 [R, R_cond], 3 [R, R_PT], 11 [P], 6 [C]; the two assumptions are listed in the register §F1, the two mathematical lemmas in a footnote; registry §08 `### Ch24`).
* **注 `rem:front-open` — 开放问题** (no tag). (1) Landau/pinch theory: remove the sign condition and the quadratic restriction of `thm:front-collins-pinch`; a refereed version; which vanishing denominators trap and the cone of allowed deformations at a pinch (needed for every approximation of Ch9); a systematic coordinate-space version. (2) Method of regions in Minkowski kinematics: a proof (`conj:front-mor-minkowski`; Jantzen's convergence step) and an algorithm guaranteed to find hidden regions (Glauber, multi-hard). (3) Glauber and factorization in hadron–hadron collisions: an [R]-level proof for inclusive DY; the fate of MPI-sensitive observables beyond pure ladders (`conj:front-mpi-violation`); PDF factorization for non-global observables beyond three loops; confirmation and resummation of the $\alpha_s^nL^{2n-2}$ coherence-violating logarithms in $N$-jettiness (consequences for $N$-jettiness slicing). (4) NLP: endpoint factorization beyond LL with incoming hadrons and a general theorem for refactorization conditions (`conj:front-nlp-beyond-ll`); Glauber cancellation at NLP (`conj:front-glauber-nlp`); reconciliation of NLP TMD results between groups. (5) BRST: the multilocal classification (`conj:front-brst-multilocal`). (6) OPE: convergence for YM/QCD (`conj:front-ope-convergence`); a self-contained flow-equation proof for QCD with quarks. (7) Positivity: a first-principles determination of the scale above which $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDFs are positive. (8) Small $x$: the origin of the Kovchegov et al. vs BER discrepancy (from four loops); a scheme-independent collinear improvement of NLO BK; consistent matching of small-$x$ and collinear factorization. (9) DPS: factorization with coloured final states (`conj:break-dps-coloured`); phenomenology of colour-correlated DPDs. (10) Quarkonium: NRQCD production factorization beyond NNLO; the "general principle" behind the two-loop confirmation. (11) Electroweak: resummation of the non-factorizing EW logarithms at $Q\gg M_W$.
* *Budget of §24.16.* 0.8 pp.

### Misconception box (Ch24)
C5 (区域方法不能用来"确认"领头区域：在 Minkowski 运动学中是循环论证；区域识别来自钳夹分析与幂次计数，`prop:front-hidden-regions`); O11 (区域方法的地位要精确：欧氏极限 [R]，Minkowski 极限无一般证明，可能需要解析正规子，`conj:front-mor-minkowski`); O12 (Landau 方程只给出奇点的必要条件；外部不变量的奇点与固定外动量下的红外发散要区分；端点与第二类奇点另需处理，`prop:front-landau-not-singular`); M2 (「PDF 是概率密度，所以 $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDF 为正」——`prop:front-msbar-negative`); M19 (「区域方法是定理」); M20 (「Landau 方程给出奇点」); M21 (「严格共线因子化的破坏说明 PDF 因子化失效」——振幅层次的陈述，`rem:front-glauber-status`); in addition 「Collins (2020) 的定理已正式发表」 and 「区域 = Newton 多面体的下表面」 (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.9).

# PART 1b — Part VII global decisions and annexes F1/F4/F5/F6 (verbatim)

## Part VII — global decisions (the former frontier §F0; binding for Ch22–Ch24)

### F0.1 Scope, budgets, format

| # | key | file | 中文标题（binding） | pp | format |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 22 | `lamet` | `ch22_lamet.tex` | LaMET 与格点因子化：季向东等人的因子化证明 | 18 | frontier |
| 23 | `yzliu` | `ch23_yzliu.tex` | Yizhuang Liu 的严格因子化研究 | 15 | frontier |
| 24 | `front` | `ch24_front.tex` | 其他前沿严格结果 | 18 | frontier |

Frontier format (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §11, §6.11): ≈55 % statements with hypotheses and proof architecture, ≈20 % explicit results (one-/two-loop kernels, checks, worked examples), ≈25 % status, evidence and open problems. Every chapter opens with the box 「本章证明了什么／没有证明什么」 (§6.6) and ends with a status table and an open-problem list. No correspondence table is required (§6.7 lists only core chapters).

### F0.2 Ownership decisions (binding for this outline)

1. **Ch22 `lamet`** owns: the LaMET principle; lattice moments and the Euclidean Compton amplitude (v1 `prop:lattice-moments`); quasi-/pseudo-distributions and lattice cross sections (v1 `def:lattice-quasi`); support and analyticity; renormalization of straight space-like Wilson-line operators (v1 `prop:lattice-renormalizability`); renormalization schemes incl. the hybrid scheme; one- and two-loop kernels; the matching theorems with the hypotheses (H1)–(H5) of the LaMET report (now `prop:lattice-renormalizability` and `asm:lamet-ope`, `asm:lamet-uniform`, `asm:lamet-twist4`, `asm:lamet-large-z`) and the three proof routes (OPE route of Izubuchi–Ji–Jin–Stewart–Zhao; Ma–Qiu collinear-subtraction route; good lattice cross sections); v1 `thm:lattice-lamet`; target-mass and dynamical power corrections; the linear renormalon of the Wilson-line self-energy and leading-renormalon resummation; the Fourier step incl. the exponential-decay theorem (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2, VF-LAM-18); LaMET vs short-distance factorization (SDF); quasi-GPD/DA; **the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, the off-light-cone and reduced soft functions (definitions), the lattice Collins–Soper kernel (v1 `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`) and TMD ratios**; v1 `rem:lattice-critiques`, `ex:lattice-quasi-tree`; the status board.
   - **Former deviation from the WRITER_SPEC_v2 v2.1 default, adopted as binding at the merge** (WRITER_SPEC_v2 v2.2 §6.11 item 5 and §12.2 now say the same; integrator decision MD9). Reason: `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` is a moved v1 `lattice` label, and the binding lead-author decision says that Ch22 absorbs the v1 `lattice` content with unchanged labels; its proof uses the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, and the no-forward-edge rule (§4.5 item 4) forbids `\uses{thm:<Ch23 label>}` in Ch22. The frontier task statement also lists "LaMET for DAs/GPDs/TMDs (Ji–Liu–Liu; Ebert–Stewart–Zhao; Vladimirov–Schäfer), lattice Collins–Soper kernel" under Ch22. Consequences: VF-LAM-15 and VF-LAM-16 are "Used in: Ch22" (`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`); `fig:yzliu-leading-region` stays in Ch23 because it illustrates Liu's Collins-style proof route (`prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`).
2. **Ch23 `yzliu`** owns: Euclidean soft functions as heavy-quark (HQET) form factors; the partial-exponentiation lemma; pinch poles and ultrasoft modes; analyticity in the complex hyperbolic angle and the universality classes; the linearity (factorization) of rapidity divergences; the intrinsic soft function $S_I$ (on-light-cone definition, scheme independence, anomalous dimension, two-loop structure); the 2024 time-ordering theorem; Liu's Collins-style power-counting proof route of the quasi-TMD theorem; the hard kernel (RGE, heavy–light decomposition, two loops); LFWF amplitudes; the meson form factor and the extraction of $S_I$; DY in terms of quasi-TMDs; Sivers ratios; the space-like jet function and its continuation; **the threshold factorization of the quasi-PDF kernel** (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2 and VF-LAM-14); the anomalous-dimension algebra; the linear renormalon of the heavy–light kernel; GPDs near $x=\pm\xi$; the exactly solvable 2D models; the extensions of the exponential-decay theorem.
3. **Ch22 does not own threshold factorization.** It owns only the one-loop endpoint structure of the matching kernel (`prop:lamet-oneloop`(e)) and the domain-of-validity remark (`rem:lamet-domain`), with forward pointers in running text to `thm:yzliu-threshold` (never in `\uses`).
4. **Ch24 `front`** owns all OTHERS.md items (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.11 item 5). It never re-states a core statement. Where a core statement already covers part of a topic, Ch24 states the frontier refinement under a new `front` label and cites the core label; the core statements keep their labels and owners. Overlaps handled this way: `thm:ir-landau-momentum`, `rem:ir-landau-status`, `prop:ir-coleman-norton`, `prop:ir-regions-minkowski`, `lem:appD-gordan` (Landau/regions); `thm:power-ope-convergence` (massive $\varphi^4_4$ OPE convergence stays in Ch21); `prop:scet-glauber-operators` (RS Glauber operators stay in Ch15); `prop:break-jet-veto`, `prop:break-fss`, `prop:break-spacelike`, `prop:break-mass-singularity`, `thm:break-double-dy` (Ch18); `prop:jets-sll`, `thm:jets-ngl-factorization`, `thm:jets-bms` (Ch16); `thm:smallx-bk`, `thm:smallx-bfkl` (Ch17); `conj:heavy-nrqcd-production`, `prop:heavy-quarkonium-fragmentation` (Ch20); `conj:pdf-positivity` (Ch8); `prop:appJ-local-operators`, `rem:appJ-nonlocal`, `prop:appL-renormalized` (App J, App L); `prop:power-nlp-endpoint` (Ch21); `prop:thr-nlp` (Ch13); `prop:jets-ngl-beyond` (Ch16); `prop:dy-abelian-model` (Ch12, the eikonal spectator phase); `thm:heavy-nrqcd-decay` (Ch20). Requested core-side pointers: §F5.
5. **Exponential decay.** Ch22 owns `thm:lamet-exp-decay` (the theorem of Ji–Liu–Su under axioms) and `conj:lamet-decay-qcd` (the QCD asymptotic forms); Ch23 owns `prop:yzliu-decay-extensions` (all-order spectral representation and exponential decay in $\varphi^3/\varphi^4$, [P]); the extension of `thm:lamet-exp-decay` to one-particle intermediate states is described in the text of that entry, with the status of `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, and is not graded separately.

### F0.4 Tags, qualifiers and assumptions in Part VII

1. **Mapping of frontier grade notations** (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 item 4): "[R] in PT, diagram by diagram" → [R, R_PT]; "[R]$_{1L}$/[R]$_{2L}$" → [R, R_PT] for the statement restricted to that order (first sentence states the order), the all-order statement keeps its own tag; "[R] (cond.)" → [R, R_cond]; "[R] under Wightman-type axioms" → [R, R_cond] with `asm:intro-wightman` + the frontier assumption it needs; "[R] (model)" → [P] with the model in the first sentence (the model-internal claim may get a full proof); "[R]†" → this outline decides (Ch24: [A] unless the proof is printed in full; status paragraph 「（证明仅见于预印本）」); sub-grades A±: dropped; "open" → [C].
2. **What becomes an `asm:`.** Only hypotheses that act as *formal premises* of a statement (R_cond premises, or hypotheses that several statements share) become register entries. The weakest *step* of an [A] argument (e.g. Liu's explicitly assumed IR-control step in the rapidity-slicing argument) is named in the 「地位与缺口」 paragraph, not turned into an assumption — otherwise the grade-propagation rule (§4.6) would turn every [A] argument with a sketched step into [C].
3. **Frontier register entries (14).** Ch22 (9): `asm:lamet-aux-renorm` [A], `asm:lamet-ope` [A], `asm:lamet-uniform` [C], `asm:lamet-twist4` [C], `asm:lamet-large-z` [C], `asm:lamet-gap` [C], `asm:lamet-staple` [A], `asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` [A], `asm:lamet-order-limits` [A]. Ch23 (3): `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair` [C], `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` [A], `asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform` [P]. Ch24 (2): `asm:front-mor-complete` [C], `asm:front-scet-modes` [A]. Full register entries with all fields: §F1. All are *technical* assumptions in the sense of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 7: they enter the grade propagation and fix the content grade of every R_cond statement that lists them (e.g. the explicitly non-perturbative `thm:lamet-exp-decay` and `cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor` have content grade [C]); only the six global `asm:intro-*` entries are exempt.
4. **Inner item names** are chapter-unique: (AX1)–(AX3) aux field; (OP1)–(OP2) OPE; (UI) uniformity; (TW1)–(TW2) twist 4; (LZ1)–(LZ2) large $z$; (GP1)–(GP3) gap; (ST1)–(ST3) staple; (SR) soft rapidity; (OL1)–(OL2) order of limits; (HP1)–(HP3) heavy pair; (WP1)–(WP2) Wightman PT; (UL) uniform light-cone limit; (MR) regions; (SM1)–(SM2) SCET modes. Local hypothesis lists inside statements: (QT1)–(QT7) (Liu's quasi-TMD power counting), (PS1)–(PS3) (positivity), (J1)–(J4) (Jantzen), (ED-a)–(ED-c) are not used (the decay theorem uses `asm:lamet-gap`). The frontier reports' own numbering (Thm 4.3, Prop 10.4, (H1)–(H6), (T1)–(T5), (Q1)–(Q7), (S1)–(S3)) is never printed.
5. **Non-perturbative status line.** Hadron-level statements end their hypotheses with 「非微扰地位：\cref{asm:…}（MA…）。」 using the global entries `asm:intro-wightman`, `asm:intro-composite`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `asm:intro-bridge`; statements about partonic states, operators or models say 「（本命题只涉及部分子态。）」 or name the model.

### F0.5 How each entry is organised

As in v1 §0.7, plus the v2 fields: **type, canonical label, Chinese title, tag (+ qualifier; for R_cond the premise sentence)**; **statement** in formulas (our conventions; VF IDs in `%` comments); **hypotheses / non-perturbative line**; **`\uses{…}`** (only statement labels, only backward edges; v2-new core labels are those of REFDOC_ANALYSIS §5 and WRITER_SPEC_v2); **proof plan** (full / sketch, numbered steps, literature locations with reading depth); **status and gap** (for [A]/[P]/[C]/[V]: weakest step, `asm:` labels, error type where power counting is involved, refereed or preprint); **verification outcome** with claim ID (verify patch v2.3); **budget** in pages. "(数学，无标签)" marks pure mathematics.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## F1. Register entries of the frontier assumptions (14)

Format of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 3: statement (the inner items are those of the chapter entry, printed once by the owner); 「地位：」; 「文献：」; 「被使用于：」 (reverse dependencies, computed from the `\uses` lists of this outline; never copied into the assumption's own `\uses`); 「解除途径：」; `\uses`. The tag is the register's and is copied by the writer, never inferred (§4.4 item 2). All 14 are technical assumptions (§4.4 item 7); the six global `asm:intro-*` entries are owned by Ch1 and are not repeated here.

### F1.1 Ch22 `lamet` (9)

1. **`asm:lamet-aux-renorm` — 含类空辅助静态色源的 QCD 的可重整性** [A] · `sec:lamet-renorm`.
   - 陈述：(AX1)–(AX3) of the Ch22 entry (auxiliary colour-triplet spinless Grassmann field $Q$ along $\hat n=\hat e_z$; renormalizability of the extended theory with $Z_Q$, $Z_1^{QQg}$ (tied to $Z_g$) and $\delta m$ only, BRST-invariantly; constant $Q$ determinant in DR).
   - 地位：[A]-level — asserted in `Ji:2017oey` (the space-like extension of the HQET renormalizability argument is stated, not written), supported by the lattice auxiliary-field construction of `Green:2017xeu` and the coordinate-space degree counting of `Ishikawa:2017faj` (superficial degrees and "bookkeeping forests", not a BPHZ proof); explicit checks: two loops `Ji:2015jwa`, three-loop anomalous dimension `Braun:2020ymy`, heavy–light current to three loops `Chetyrkin:2003vi`. Decision D3 / A26: the multiplicative renormalizability of space-like Wilson-line operators is [A] in this book.
   - 文献：`Ji:2017oey`, `Green:2017xeu`, `Ishikawa:2017faj`, `Dorn:1986dt`, `Dotsenko:1979wb`, `Braun:2020ymy`, `Chetyrkin:2003vi`.
   - 被使用于：`prop:lattice-renormalizability`, `asm:lamet-ope`, `asm:lamet-gap`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`, `asm:lamet-staple`, `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet`.
   - 解除途径：a written BRST-invariant renormalization proof of QCD + space-like static source (Zimmermann/BPHZ with the anisotropic power counting of the propagator $\theta(x^z)\delta(x^0)\delta^2(x_\perp)$, or flow equations in the style of `Frob:2015uqy`), including the treatment of $\det(i\hat n\cdot D)$ and of $\delta(0)$-type terms.
   - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst, def:prelim-wilson}`.
2. **`asm:lamet-ope` — 类空 Wilson 线算符的短距离 OPE（重整化微扰论）** [A] · `sec:lattice-matching`.
   - 陈述：(OP1) the leading-twist light-ray OPE of the renormalized operator $O^R_{\gamma^\alpha}(z,\mu)$ as $z^2\to0$ at fixed $\lambda$, with traceless symmetric twist-2 quark and gluon operators and coefficients $C_n$, $C'_n$; (OP2) for massless on-shell partonic states the leading-twist expression holds for all $z$ order by order.
   - 地位：[A] — argued by `IzubuchiEtAl2018` (read) from the auxiliary-field localization (the non-local operator becomes a product of local operators of the extended theory) plus the standard OPE; no Zimmermann-level theorem for Wilson-line operators is known — wording 「据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索，尚未见……」 (A06). The remark under `tab:lamet-dependency` lists what a proof needs.
   - 文献：`IzubuchiEtAl2018` §II, `Radyushkin2017`, `Braun:2007wv`, `Ji:2017oey`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-coordinate`, `thm:lamet-parton-momentum`, `prop:lamet-tmc`, `prop:lamet-sdf`, `thm:lamet-quasi-gpd`.
   - 解除途径：Zimmermann's forest formula for the extended theory with the anisotropic power counting; or a flow-equation OPE theorem (cf. `Frob:2016mzv`) extended to theories with a static auxiliary field.
   - `\uses{prop:lattice-renormalizability, asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:dis-twist}`.
3. **`asm:lamet-uniform` — 扭度展开对 Ioffe 时间的一致性** [C] · `sec:lamet-power`.
   - 陈述：(UI-a) coordinate form — the twist-2 remainder of the hadronic ITD is bounded by $K(-z^2)\Lambda^2(1+|\ln(-z^2\mu^2)|)^k$ uniformly in $\lambda$ for $0<-z^2\le z_0^2$; (UI-b) ray form — along $z^2=-\lambda^2/P_z^2$, $|\lambda|\le z_0P^z$, the remainder beyond twist 4 has Fourier transform $o(P_z^{-2})$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$.
   - 地位：[C] — no all-order argument; evidence from the renormalon model (`prop:lamet-bvz`) and from solvable models; Liu's Gross–Neveu theorem covers only imaginary Ioffe time (`thm:yzliu-gn-convergence`, Ch23). The frontier report's hypothesis was reformulated (writer note in the Ch22 entry): a pointwise bound of type (UI-a) does not give a $P^z$-suppressed Fourier transform.
   - 文献：`IzubuchiEtAl2018`, `Braun:2018brg`, `JiEtAl2021`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-hadronic`.
   - 解除途径：a non-perturbative OPE with remainder bounds uniform in $\lambda$ (cf. `asm:intro-np-ope`); as a first step a model proof at real Ioffe time.
   - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, prop:lamet-tmc}`.
4. **`asm:lamet-twist4` — 领头幂次修正的结构（扭度 4）** [C] · `sec:lamet-power`.
   - 陈述：(TW1) $\tilde h_{\rm tw4}=z^2\Lambda^2\int_{-1}^1d\alpha\,e^{i\alpha\lambda}\rho_4(\alpha,z^2)$ with $\rho_4$ supported in $[-1,1]$, smooth inside; (TW2) at most logarithmic $z^2$ dependence of $\rho_4$.
   - 地位：[C] — the form is suggested by the twist-4 OPE in perturbation theory (support as in `prop:lamet-support`), the hadronic structure is assumed.
   - 文献：`Braun:2018brg`, `Radyushkin2017`, `JiEtAl2021`.
   - 被使用于：`asm:lamet-large-z`, `thm:lamet-hadronic`.
   - 解除途径：a twist-4 OPE for hadron matrix elements with control of the $z^2$ dependence (non-perturbative OPE).
   - `\uses{prop:lamet-support, cor:lamet-entire}`.
5. **`asm:lamet-large-z` — 大距离区域的控制** [C] · `sec:lamet-power`.
   - 陈述：(LZ1) the region $|\lambda|>z_0P^z$ contributes $o(1)$ to the Fourier transform of $\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$; (LZ2) the same with $\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}-\tilde h_{\rm tw4}$ is $o(P_z^{-2})$.
   - 地位：[C] — requires large-distance control of hadronic Wilson-line correlators with constants uniform in $P^z$; `thm:lamet-exp-decay` gives decay under `asm:lamet-gap` but no usable constants; `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`.
   - 文献：`Ji:2026vir` (预印本), `JiEtAl2021`, `Gao:2021dbh`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-hadronic`.
   - 解除途径：quantitative exponential decay of hadronic matrix elements with constants uniform in $P^z$.
   - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, asm:lamet-twist4}`.
6. **`asm:lamet-gap` — 谱隙与边界值正则性** [C] · `sec:lamet-fourier`.
   - 陈述：(GP1) isolated one-particle external state, other contributing states at least $\Delta m$ above it; (GP2) Fredenhagen-type regularity of the boundary values of the ordered two-point functions in complex time; (GP3) QCD application: non-perturbative reduction of the Wilson-line operator to local operators of QCD + static source and a gap $\Lambda_X$ above the static threshold.
   - 地位：[C] for QCD — (GP1), (GP2) are standard axiomatic hypotheses for the channel considered; (GP3) needs confinement in the heavy–light sector and a non-perturbative version of `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`; lattice values ($\Lambda_X\approx0.5$ GeV) are evidence only.
   - 文献：`Ji:2026vir` §5 (预印本, read), `Fredenhagen:1984bz`.
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`.
   - 解除途径：(GP1), (GP2) in a constructive or axiomatic setting for the channel; (GP3) requires control of confinement — beyond present methods.
   - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm}`.
7. **`asm:lamet-staple` — 订书钉形类空 Wilson 线算符的重整化与 $L\to\infty$ 极限** [A] · `sec:lattice-tmd`.
   - 陈述：(ST1) multiplicative renormalizability of the staple operator divided by $\sqrt{Z_E}$ with end-point factors only; (ST2) existence of $L\to\infty$ order by order (pinch poles cancel against $\sqrt{Z_E}$; ultrasoft modes cancel after resummation of dipole self-interactions); (ST3) on the lattice $\sqrt{Z_E}$ plus the short-distance matrix element removes all UV divergences; same-dimension mixing as in `prop:lattice-renormalizability`(b).
   - 地位：[A]-level — argued to all orders (Ch23 `prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft` gives the all-order argument for (ST2), itself [A] with the ultrasoft cancellation argued, not proven); explicit one-loop checks; lattice renormalization studies.
   - 文献：`Ji:2019ewn`, `Liu:2022nnk` §§3–4, `Ebert:2022fmh`, `Ebert:2019tvc`, `Constantinou:2019vyb`, `Alexandrou:2023ucc`; history `Collins:2008ht`, `Ji:2018hvs` (A04).
   - 被使用于：`def:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`.
   - 解除途径：the auxiliary-field renormalization proof (as for `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`) for staples with cusps, plus an all-order treatment of ultrasoft and "ultra-ultrasoft" modes.
   - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:prelim-wilson}`.
8. **`asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` — 离光锥软函数的大快度结构** [A] · `sec:lattice-tmd`.
   - 陈述：(SR) $\ln S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)+\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$ with $\tilde K$ the Collins–Soper kernel.
   - 地位：[A]-level — the all-order argument is Ch23 `prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear` (complete except an explicitly assumed IR step of the rapidity slicing); claimed in `CollinsSoper1981`; conformal-map renormalization theorem for light-like regulators (`Vladimirov:2017ksc`; Ch24 `prop:front-rapidity-renorm`); explicit three-loop soft functions (`Li:2016axz`).
   - 文献：`Ji:2019sxk`, `Liu:2022nnk` App. E, `CollinsSoper1981`, `Vladimirov:2017ksc`, `Li:2016axz`.
   - 被使用于：`def:lamet-reduced-soft`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`.
   - 解除途径：a proof of the IR-control step of the rapidity-slicing argument (Ch23).
   - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, prop:tmd-evolution}`.
9. **`asm:lamet-order-limits` — 极限次序** [A] · `sec:lattice-tmd`.
   - 陈述：(OL1) $\varepsilon\to0$ commutes with $L\to\infty$ and with the off-light-cone rapidity regulator limit for $\Omega/\sqrt S$; (OL2) the large-rapidity and Collins schemes differ only in the order of $\varepsilon\to0$ and $y_B\to-\infty$, a UV effect absorbed into $C_q(\sqrt{\zeta_{\rm LR}}/2,\mu)$.
   - 地位：[A] — argued to all orders by `Ebert:2022fmh` ((OL1) verified at one loop; (OL2) by asymptotic freedom), not a diagram-by-diagram proof.
   - 文献：`Ebert:2022fmh` (read).
   - 被使用于：`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`.
   - 解除途径：a diagrammatic proof of (OL1) beyond one loop and of (OL2).
   - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:tmd-collins}`.

### F1.2 Ch23 `yzliu` (3)

10. **`asm:yzliu-heavy-pair` — 静态色单态 $Q\bar Q$ 对的谱假设** [C] · `sec:yzliu-soft`.
    - 陈述：(HP1) isolated colour-singlet ground state of the static pair at fixed $\vec b_\perp$ with residual energy $\gamma^{-1}V(\vec b_\perp)$, gapped continuum; (HP2) non-zero, boost-invariant overlap of the pair interpolating field; (HP3) Wilson-line self-energies and the pair time evolution cancel against $\sqrt{ZZ}$ (LSZ-type amputation; perturbatively `lem:yzliu-partial-exp`).
    - 地位：[C] — non-perturbative spectral assumptions; (HP1) is supported by lattice static potentials (evidence).
    - 文献：`Ji:2019sxk` (read), `Liu:2022nnk` §4.1–4.2, App. A.
    - 被使用于：`cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor`.
    - 解除途径：(HP1) from a gap in the static colour-singlet sector (confinement); (HP2), (HP3) from an LSZ-type construction for static sources in non-perturbative HQET.
    - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc}`.
11. **`asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` — 维数正规化微扰论中的 Wightman 结构** [A] · `sec:yzliu-analyticity`.
    - 陈述：(WP1) order by order, in linear covariant gauges and DR at generic $\varepsilon\ne0$, gluon and ghost Wightman functions satisfy the spectral condition and local commutativity, so that their coordinate-space representations are boundary values of functions holomorphic in the (permuted extended) tubes, covariant under proper complex Lorentz transformations; (WP2) null separations on the same light-like line give scaleless contributions, so $\eta\to0^+$ may be taken at integrand level.
    - 地位：[A]-level — formally a consequence of the covariant operator formalism (`KugoOjima1979`) and of the Bargmann–Hall–Wightman theorem (`Streater:1989vi`), used by Liu ("the author believes in it", `Liu:2023pjb` §4 item 2); not proven as a precise statement for DR at generic $\varepsilon$ (indefinite metric, gauge-dependent Green functions). It is the bottleneck of `thm:yzliu-analyticity`, `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`, `thm:yzliu-jet-continuation`.
    - 文献：`Liu:2022nnk` App. F, `Liu:2023pjb` §2 and App. B, `Ji:2023pba` App. C.2, `KugoOjima1979`, `Streater:1989vi`.
    - 被使用于：`thm:yzliu-analyticity`, `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`, `conj:yzliu-complex-wilson`, `thm:yzliu-jet-continuation`.
    - 解除途径：a proof that the DR parametric representations of all gluon/ghost Wightman functions with numerators are boundary values of holomorphic functions in the permuted extended tubes — e.g. by extending the positivity of $P_{ij}(\alpha)$ (`lem:yzliu-matrix-tree`) and the below-threshold argument of `thm:yzliu-time-ordering` to all graphs.
    - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst}`.
12. **`asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform` — 光锥极限对 ${\rm Im}\,\theta$ 的一致性** [P] · `sec:yzliu-rapidity`.
    - 陈述：(UL) the light-cone limit $Y\to\infty$ of the single-time-ordered soft functions is uniform in ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in[-\pi,\pi]$.
    - 地位：[P] — verified perturbatively for ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in\{0,\pm\frac\pi2,\pm\pi\}$ only.
    - 文献：`Liu:2022nnk` §4.5–4.7, §6.
    - 被使用于：`prop:yzliu-universality-classes`.
    - 解除途径：a uniform bound on the $O(e^{-\theta})$ remainder in the strip at each order.
    - `\uses{def:yzliu-hyperbolic-angle, thm:yzliu-analyticity}`.

### F1.3 Ch24 `front` (2)

13. **`asm:front-mor-complete` — 区域集合的完备性** [C] · `sec:front-regions`.
    - 陈述：(MR) for the expansion considered, the listed regions (essential facets, or the regions found after dissecting the polytope), each expanded and integrated over the whole domain, reproduce the asymptotic expansion to all orders — no hidden regions, no non-scaleless overlap contributions beyond those of `lem:front-jantzen`.
    - 地位：[C] — proven for Euclidean limits (`thm:ir-regions-euclid`) and for the one-facet case (`prop:front-lp-facets`); the facet recipe fails in Minkowski kinematics from three loops (`prop:front-hidden-regions`); no general argument.
    - 文献：`Semenova:2018cwy`, `Gardi:2022khw` §6, `Gardi:2024axt`, `Jantzen2011`.
    - 被使用于：`prop:front-commuting`.
    - 解除途径：`conj:front-mor-minkowski`; an algorithm guaranteed to find all regions (dissecting the polytope) with a completeness proof.
    - `\uses{def:front-facets, def:ir-regions}`.
14. **`asm:front-scet-modes` — SCET 的模式完备性与 Glauber 模式的缺席** [A] · `sec:front-nlp`.
    - 陈述：(SM1) the listed hard, (anti-)hard-collinear, collinear, soft and soft-quark modes reproduce all regions at the stated power (zero-bin subtractions consistent); (SM2) Glauber modes do not contribute at that power (automatic for decays into colour-neutral states and for $e^+e^-$; for partonic coefficient functions of processes with incoming partons the statement concerns the partonic cross section only).
    - 地位：[A] — the standard SCET assumption (critic C6, M18), covered by the region analyses of the processes considered; `prop:front-hidden-regions` shows that completeness has to be checked case by case. Ownership note (§F5 item 11): it may move to Ch15 if the lead author wants `thm:scet-thrust` to cite it.
    - 文献：`Liu:2019oav`, `Liu:2020wbn`, `Beneke:2022obx`, `RothsteinStewart2016`.
    - 被使用于：`thm:front-hgg`, `prop:front-ggh`, `prop:front-nlp-ll`, `prop:front-nlp-dis`, `prop:front-sidis-nlp`.
    - 解除途径：a region analysis (pinch analysis and power counting, Ch4, Ch9) of each process at the stated power with the Glauber region treated explicitly.
    - `\uses{def:scet-modes}`.
