# PART 4 — Assumption register entries used (ASSUMPTIONS.md §3, verbatim)

#### `asm:front-mor-complete` — 区域集合的完备性 [\tagC]
- **所属：** Ch24 `sec:front-regions`. Technical.
- 陈述：(MR) for the expansion considered, the listed regions (essential facets, or the regions found after dissecting the polytope), each expanded and integrated over the whole domain, reproduce the asymptotic expansion to all orders — no hidden regions, no non-scaleless overlap contributions beyond those of `lem:front-jantzen`.
- 地位：[C] — proven for Euclidean limits (`thm:ir-regions-euclid`) and for the one-facet case (`prop:front-lp-facets`); the facet recipe fails in Minkowski kinematics from three loops (`prop:front-hidden-regions`); no general argument.
- 文献：`Semenova:2018cwy`, `Gardi:2022khw` §6, `Gardi:2024axt`, `Jantzen2011`.
- 被使用于：`prop:front-commuting` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：`conj:front-mor-minkowski`; an algorithm guaranteed to find all regions (dissecting the polytope) with a completeness proof.
- `\uses{def:front-facets, def:ir-regions}`.

#### `asm:front-scet-modes` — SCET 的模式完备性与 Glauber 模式的缺席 [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch24 `sec:front-nlp`. Technical.
- 陈述：(SM1) the listed hard, (anti-)hard-collinear, collinear, soft and soft-quark modes reproduce all regions at the stated power (zero-bin subtractions consistent); (SM2) Glauber modes do not contribute at that power (automatic for decays into colour-neutral states and for $e^+e^-$; for partonic coefficient functions of processes with incoming partons the statement concerns the partonic cross section only).
- 地位：[A] — the standard SCET assumption (critic C6, M18), covered by the region analyses of the processes considered; `prop:front-hidden-regions` shows that completeness has to be checked case by case. Ownership note (§F5 item 11): it may move to Ch15 if the lead author wants `thm:scet-thrust` to cite it.
- 文献：`Liu:2019oav`, `Liu:2020wbn`, `Beneke:2022obx`, `RothsteinStewart2016`.
- 被使用于：`thm:front-hgg`, `prop:front-ggh`, `prop:front-nlp-ll`, `prop:front-nlp-dis`, `prop:front-sidis-nlp` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a region analysis (pinch analysis and power counting, Ch4, Ch9) of each process at the stated power with the Glauber region treated explicitly.
- `\uses{def:scet-modes}`.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#### `asm:intro-` — (no full entry found in ASSUMPTIONS.md; see FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 owner entry)
#### `asm:intro-bridge` — 从部分子微扰论到强子的桥接假设 [\tagC] (global)
- **所属：** Ch1 `sec:intro-tags`; technical commentary (which axiom is used where) in App M §M.8. Global (not in the grade propagation of ordinary statements).
- **Binding text** (FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 Ch1 entry, reproduced verbatim; the 「被使用于」 field there lists the direct `\uses` users computed at the merge):
    - **陈述.** (Br1) *Region completeness:* the leading-power regions found in PT for IR-regulated partonic states are the only leading-power regions in QCD with hadron states; confinement modifies only the IR-sensitive factors and creates no new leading region. (Br2) *Power-counting transfer:* all $\Lambda$-sensitivity of a hadronic cross section covered by a factorization theorem sits in the hadronic matrix elements of the same operators (PDFs, FFs, TMDs, DAs, soft factors) that appear in the partonic theorem. (Br3) *Asymptoticity:* the remainder is $O((\Lambda/Q)^p)$ for some $p>0$ as an asymptotic statement; perturbative series are asymptotic and renormalon ambiguities are of the order of power corrections, so "exact up to power corrections" has meaning only once the power-correction term is defined.
    - **地位：** open; supported by phenomenology, lattice consistency and renormalon analyses; the value of $p$ is not fixed by the perturbative proofs except for OPE-accessible quantities. **文献：** `CollinsSoperSterman1989`, `Collins2011` (ch. 14 discussion), `Beneke1999`. **被使用于：** direct `\uses` users (computed at the merge): `thm:method-template`, `thm:disall-factorization`, `thm:disall-heavy`, `prop:disall-nuclear`, `thm:coll-sia`, `thm:coll-sidis`, `thm:coll-direct-photo`, `thm:dy-factorization`, `thm:dy-colour-singlet`, `thm:dy-polarized`, `thm:dy-higher-twist`, `thm:tmd-factorization`, `prop:tmd-small-b`, `cor:tmd-sivers-observable`, `prop:tmd-collins-universality`, `thm:scet-thrust`, `thm:jets-ngl-factorization`, `thm:break-ddis`, `thm:break-double-dy`, `thm:excl-pion-ff`, `thm:excl-transition`, `thm:excl-dvcs`, `thm:excl-meson-L`, `thm:heavy-bdpi`, `prop:heavy-btopi-ff`, `prop:heavy-qcdf-lp`, `thm:heavy-inclusive-b`, `thm:heavy-nrqcd-decay`, `prop:heavy-quarkonium-fragmentation`, `prop:power-dis-twist4-x`; in addition every hadron-level statement whose 「非微扰地位」 line names it (`def:intro-MA` refers to all six global entries). **解除途径：** non-perturbative control of QCD (Ch25 open problem OP-A1).
    - `\uses{def:intro-factorization}`

#### `asm:intro-composite` — 规范不变复合算符与光线算符的存在与重整化群 [\tagC] (global)
- **所属：** Ch1 `sec:intro-tags`; technical commentary (which axiom is used where) in App M §M.8. Global (not in the grade propagation of ordinary statements).
- **Binding text** (FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 Ch1 entry, reproduced verbatim; the 「被使用于」 field there lists the direct `\uses` users computed at the merge):
    - **陈述.** (C1) Gauge-invariant local composite operators exist as Wightman fields (in the sense of `asm:intro-wightman`) and are organized in finite families graded by mass dimension; after renormalization they obey RG equations with triangular mixing (an operator mixes only with operators of equal or lower dimension), with $\beta$ and anomalous-dimension matrices asymptotic to their perturbative series. (C2) The spin-$n$ twist-2 sector with non-zero spin-averaged forward matrix elements is spanned by the quark operators $O_q^{(n)}$ (one per flavour) and the gluon operator $O_g^{(n)}$ (even $n$) of the perturbative classification (forward pointers: `def:dis-twist`, `lem:dis-twist-classification`). (C3) The PDF light-ray operators (Wilson-line bilocals of `def:pdf-lightray`, forward pointer) exist between hadron states as tempered distributions in $w^-$, and their moments are the reduced matrix elements $A_i^{(n)}$ of the local twist-2 operators.
    - **地位：** open (presupposes `asm:intro-wightman`); in PT, (C1)–(C3) are theorems (`thm:prelim-composite-renorm`, `thm:pdf-moments-local`). **文献：** `Joglekar:1976eb`, `Joglekar:1976pe`, `Hollands:2007zg` (PT/pure YM). **被使用于：** direct `\uses` users (computed at the merge): `asm:intro-np-ope`, `prop:dis-ope-rg`, `thm:dis-moments-np`, `cor:dis-weak-xspace`, `thm:dis-moments`, `prop:dis-twist4`, `prop:dis-tmc`, `thm:dis-bjorken`, `prop:pdf-invariance`, `prop:pdf-support-hadron`, `thm:pdf-moments-local`, `thm:pdf-dglap`, `thm:pdf-dis-xspace`, `prop:pdf-sum-rules`, `prop:pdf-timelike-dglap`, `prop:coll-photon-sf`, `prop:coll-luxqed`, `thm:coll-g1-moments`, `prop:coll-transversity-evolution`, `prop:tmd-small-b`, `thm:tmd-sivers-sign`, `prop:excl-erbl`, `prop:excl-polynomiality`, `thm:heavy-inclusive-b`, `lem:appM-norm-bound`; in addition every hadron-level statement whose 「非微扰地位」 line names it (`def:intro-MA` refers to all six global entries). **解除途径：** as for `asm:intro-wightman`.
    - `\uses{asm:intro-wightman}`

#### `asm:intro-np-ope` — 非微扰算符乘积展开 [\tagC] (global)
- **所属：** Ch1 `sec:intro-tags`; technical commentary (which axiom is used where) in App M §M.8. Global (not in the grade propagation of ordinary statements).
- **Binding text** (FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 Ch1 entry, reproduced verbatim; the 「被使用于」 field there lists the direct `\uses` users computed at the merge):
    - **陈述.** (O1) For the T-product of two currents, the Euclidean OPE holds between the vacuum and between the hadron states of `asm:intro-wightman`, to every finite level $N$ in the operator dimension: $\tilde T^{\mu\nu}(q)-\sum_{d_j\le N}\tilde C^{\mu\nu}_j(q;\mu)\langle O_j\rangle=O(|q|^{2-N-\delta}\ln^k|q|)$, with remainder bounds uniform in the direction of $q$ and with bounds on all $q$-derivatives; the coefficients are Lorentz covariant. (O2) Odd-twist (chiral-odd) coefficients are suppressed by a light-quark mass ($\propto m_q/|q|$).
    - **地位：** open, widely assumed; its vacuum part is `conj:ee-np-ope` [C] (forward pointer); compatible with renormalons and instantons only as an asymptotic statement (`asm:intro-af-coeff`). **文献：** `Wilson1969`, `ShifmanVainshteinZakharov1979a`, `Novikov:1984rf`; AQFT approach `Bostelmann:2005rr`. **被使用于：** direct `\uses` users (computed at the merge): `asm:intro-af-coeff`, `lem:dis-ope-segment`, `thm:dis-fixed-n`, `thm:dis-moments-np`, `cor:dis-weak-xspace`, `thm:dis-moments`, `prop:dis-twist4`, `prop:dis-tmc`, `thm:dis-bjorken`, `thm:pdf-dis-xspace`, `prop:coll-photon-sf`, `thm:coll-g1-moments`; in addition every hadron-level statement whose 「非微扰地位」 line names it (`def:intro-MA` refers to all six global entries). **解除途径：** a non-perturbative definition of the OPE in QCD (Ch25 open problem OP-A3).
    - `\uses{asm:intro-wightman, asm:intro-composite}`

#### `asm:intro-wightman` — QCD 的 Wightman 结构 [\tagC] (global)
- **所属：** Ch1 `sec:intro-tags`; technical commentary (which axiom is used where) in App M §M.8. Global (not in the grade propagation of ordinary statements).
- **Binding text** (FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 Ch1 entry, reproduced verbatim; the 「被使用于」 field there lists the direct `\uses` users computed at the merge):
    - **陈述.** (W1) QCD with $n_f$ quark flavours of positive masses exists as a Wightman theory: a Hilbert space with positive metric, a strongly continuous unitary representation of the Poincaré group, a unique invariant vacuum $|0\rangle$, and local gauge-invariant composite fields — in particular the conserved hermitian electromagnetic current $j^\mu$ and the operators of `asm:intro-composite` — as operator-valued tempered distributions satisfying locality; parity is a symmetry. (W2) Spectral condition: the joint spectrum of $P^\mu$ lies in the closed forward cone; **in the superselection sector of the target's conserved flavour quantum numbers** the spectrum lies in $\{p^2\ge M^2,p^0>0\}$, $M$ the target mass, and for Haag–Ruelle one-particle states $M$ is an isolated eigenvalue of the mass operator in that sector (isolation is used only for the existence of the asymptotic target state). (W3) The connected forward matrix elements $\langle P|j^\mu(x)j^\nu(0)|P\rangle$ are tempered distributions in $x$. (W4) A covariant retarded extension $\tilde R^{\mu\nu}(q)$ of the commutator exists with $q_\mu\tilde R^{\mu\nu}=0$; its polynomial (contact-term) ambiguity affects only finitely many low Taylor coefficients $t_n$ of the forward amplitude. Massive light quarks are part of the assumption; the reconciliation with massless $\overline{\rm MS}$ coefficients is part of `asm:intro-af-coeff`.
    - **地位：** open — 4D QCD has not been constructed (the existence of 4D Yang–Mills with a mass gap is a Clay Millennium problem); widely assumed; (W2) as stated here corrects the spectral condition "nucleon isolated below the $N\pi$ continuum", which is false in the chiral limit and for $m_u\ne m_d$ (ERR-M25). **文献：** `Streater:1989vi`, `Jost:1965yxu`, `Haag:1958vt`; technical commentary (which axiom is used where) in App M §M.8. **被使用于：** direct `\uses` users (computed at the merge): `asm:intro-composite`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `asm:intro-growth`, `prop:prelim-optical`, `prop:ee-R-ImPi`, `prop:ee-analytic`, `prop:ee-smearing`, `prop:dis-W-structure`, `prop:dis-ope-rg`, `prop:dis-dispersion-np`, `thm:dis-fixed-n`, `thm:dis-moments-np`, `cor:dis-weak-xspace`, `thm:dis-moments`, `prop:dis-twist4`, `prop:dis-tmc`, `thm:dis-bjorken`, `prop:pdf-invariance`, `prop:pdf-support-hadron`, `thm:pdf-moments-local`, `thm:pdf-dis-xspace`, `prop:pdf-sum-rules`, `prop:pdf-timelike-dglap`, `prop:coll-photon-sf`, `prop:coll-luxqed`, `thm:coll-g1-moments`, `prop:coll-transversity-evolution`, `thm:tmd-sivers-sign`, `prop:excl-erbl`, `prop:excl-polynomiality`, `thm:heavy-inclusive-b`, `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `lem:appM-forward-tube`, `lem:appM-positivity`; in addition every hadron-level statement whose 「非微扰地位」 line names it (`def:intro-MA` refers to all six global entries). **解除途径：** construction of QCD (constructive field theory); in the meantime: lattice QCD as non-perturbative evidence (not proof).
    - `% uses: none`

#### `asm:lamet-aux-renorm` — 含类空辅助静态色源的 QCD 的可重整性 [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lamet-renorm`. Technical.
- 陈述：(AX1)–(AX3) of the Ch22 entry (auxiliary colour-triplet spinless Grassmann field $Q$ along $\hat n=\hat e_z$; renormalizability of the extended theory with $Z_Q$, $Z_1^{QQg}$ (tied to $Z_g$) and $\delta m$ only, BRST-invariantly; constant $Q$ determinant in DR).
- 地位：[A]-level — asserted in `Ji:2017oey` (the space-like extension of the HQET renormalizability argument is stated, not written), supported by the lattice auxiliary-field construction of `Green:2017xeu` and the coordinate-space degree counting of `Ishikawa:2017faj` (superficial degrees and "bookkeeping forests", not a BPHZ proof); explicit checks: two loops `Ji:2015jwa`, three-loop anomalous dimension `Braun:2020ymy`, heavy–light current to three loops `Chetyrkin:2003vi`. Decision D3 / A26: the multiplicative renormalizability of space-like Wilson-line operators is [A] in this book.
- 文献：`Ji:2017oey`, `Green:2017xeu`, `Ishikawa:2017faj`, `Dorn:1986dt`, `Dotsenko:1979wb`, `Braun:2020ymy`, `Chetyrkin:2003vi`.
- 被使用于：`prop:lattice-renormalizability`, `asm:lamet-ope`, `asm:lamet-gap`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`, `asm:lamet-staple`, `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a written BRST-invariant renormalization proof of QCD + space-like static source (Zimmermann/BPHZ with the anisotropic power counting of the propagator $\theta(x^z)\delta(x^0)\delta^2(x_\perp)$, or flow equations in the style of `Frob:2015uqy`), including the treatment of $\det(i\hat n\cdot D)$ and of $\delta(0)$-type terms.
- `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst, def:prelim-wilson}`.

#### `asm:lamet-gap` — 谱隙与边界值正则性 [\tagC]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lamet-fourier`. Technical.
- 陈述：(GP1) isolated one-particle external state, other contributing states at least $\Delta m$ above it; (GP2) Fredenhagen-type regularity of the boundary values of the ordered two-point functions in complex time; (GP3) QCD application: non-perturbative reduction of the Wilson-line operator to local operators of QCD + static source and a gap $\Lambda_X$ above the static threshold.
- 地位：[C] for QCD — (GP1), (GP2) are standard axiomatic hypotheses for the channel considered; (GP3) needs confinement in the heavy–light sector and a non-perturbative version of `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`; lattice values ($\Lambda_X\approx0.5$ GeV) are evidence only.
- 文献：`Ji:2026vir` §5 (预印本, read), `Fredenhagen:1984bz`.
- 被使用于：`thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：(GP1), (GP2) in a constructive or axiomatic setting for the channel; (GP3) requires control of confinement — beyond present methods.
- `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm}`.

#### `asm:lamet-large-z` — 大距离区域的控制 [\tagC]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lamet-power`. Technical.
- 陈述：(LZ1) the region $|\lambda|>z_0P^z$ contributes $o(1)$ to the Fourier transform of $\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$; (LZ2) the same with $\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}-\tilde h_{\rm tw4}$ is $o(P_z^{-2})$.
- 地位：[C] — requires large-distance control of hadronic Wilson-line correlators with constants uniform in $P^z$; `thm:lamet-exp-decay` gives decay under `asm:lamet-gap` but no usable constants; `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`.
- 文献：`Ji:2026vir` (预印本), `JiEtAl2021`, `Gao:2021dbh`.
- 被使用于：`thm:lamet-hadronic` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：quantitative exponential decay of hadronic matrix elements with constants uniform in $P^z$.
- `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, asm:lamet-twist4}`.

#### `asm:lamet-ope` — 类空 Wilson 线算符的短距离 OPE（重整化微扰论） [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lattice-matching`. Technical.
- 陈述：(OP1) the leading-twist light-ray OPE of the renormalized operator $O^R_{\gamma^\alpha}(z,\mu)$ as $z^2\to0$ at fixed $\lambda$, with traceless symmetric twist-2 quark and gluon operators and coefficients $C_n$, $C'_n$; (OP2) for massless on-shell partonic states the leading-twist expression holds for all $z$ order by order.
- 地位：[A] — argued by `IzubuchiEtAl2018` (read) from the auxiliary-field localization (the non-local operator becomes a product of local operators of the extended theory) plus the standard OPE; no Zimmermann-level theorem for Wilson-line operators is known — wording 「据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索，尚未见……」 (A06). The remark under `tab:lamet-dependency` lists what a proof needs.
- 文献：`IzubuchiEtAl2018` §II, `Radyushkin2017`, `Braun:2007wv`, `Ji:2017oey`.
- 被使用于：`thm:lamet-coordinate`, `thm:lamet-parton-momentum`, `prop:lamet-tmc`, `prop:lamet-sdf`, `thm:lamet-quasi-gpd` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：Zimmermann's forest formula for the extended theory with the anisotropic power counting; or a flow-equation OPE theorem (cf. `Frob:2016mzv`) extended to theories with a static auxiliary field.
- `\uses{prop:lattice-renormalizability, asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:dis-twist}`.

#### `asm:lamet-order-limits` — 极限次序 [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lattice-tmd`. Technical.
- 陈述：(OL1) $\varepsilon\to0$ commutes with $L\to\infty$ and with the off-light-cone rapidity regulator limit for $\Omega/\sqrt S$; (OL2) the large-rapidity and Collins schemes differ only in the order of $\varepsilon\to0$ and $y_B\to-\infty$, a UV effect absorbed into $C_q(\sqrt{\zeta_{\rm LR}}/2,\mu)$.
- 地位：[A] — argued to all orders by `Ebert:2022fmh` ((OL1) verified at one loop; (OL2) by asymptotic freedom), not a diagram-by-diagram proof.
- 文献：`Ebert:2022fmh` (read).
- 被使用于：`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a diagrammatic proof of (OL1) beyond one loop and of (OL2).
- `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:tmd-collins}`.

#### `asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` — 离光锥软函数的大快度结构 [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lattice-tmd`. Technical.
- 陈述：(SR) $\ln S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)+\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$ with $\tilde K$ the Collins–Soper kernel.
- 地位：[A]-level — the all-order argument is Ch23 `prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear` (complete except an explicitly assumed IR step of the rapidity slicing); claimed in `CollinsSoper1981`; conformal-map renormalization theorem for light-like regulators (`Vladimirov:2017ksc`; Ch24 `prop:front-rapidity-renorm`); explicit three-loop soft functions (`Li:2016axz`).
- 文献：`Ji:2019sxk`, `Liu:2022nnk` App. E, `CollinsSoper1981`, `Vladimirov:2017ksc`, `Li:2016axz`.
- 被使用于：`def:lamet-reduced-soft`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a proof of the IR-control step of the rapidity-slicing argument (Ch23).
- `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, prop:tmd-evolution}`.

#### `asm:lamet-staple` — 订书钉形类空 Wilson 线算符的重整化与 $L\to\infty$ 极限 [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lattice-tmd`. Technical.
- 陈述：(ST1) multiplicative renormalizability of the staple operator divided by $\sqrt{Z_E}$ with end-point factors only; (ST2) existence of $L\to\infty$ order by order (pinch poles cancel against $\sqrt{Z_E}$; ultrasoft modes cancel after resummation of dipole self-interactions); (ST3) on the lattice $\sqrt{Z_E}$ plus the short-distance matrix element removes all UV divergences; same-dimension mixing as in `prop:lattice-renormalizability`(b).
- 地位：[A]-level — argued to all orders (Ch23 `prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft` gives the all-order argument for (ST2), itself [A] with the ultrasoft cancellation argued, not proven); explicit one-loop checks; lattice renormalization studies.
- 文献：`Ji:2019ewn`, `Liu:2022nnk` §§3–4, `Ebert:2022fmh`, `Ebert:2019tvc`, `Constantinou:2019vyb`, `Alexandrou:2023ucc`; history `Collins:2008ht`, `Ji:2018hvs` (A04).
- 被使用于：`def:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：the auxiliary-field renormalization proof (as for `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`) for staples with cusps, plus an all-order treatment of ultrasoft and "ultra-ultrasoft" modes.
- `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:prelim-wilson}`.

#### `asm:lamet-twist4` — 领头幂次修正的结构（扭度 4） [\tagC]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lamet-power`. Technical.
- 陈述：(TW1) $\tilde h_{\rm tw4}=z^2\Lambda^2\int_{-1}^1d\alpha\,e^{i\alpha\lambda}\rho_4(\alpha,z^2)$ with $\rho_4$ supported in $[-1,1]$, smooth inside; (TW2) at most logarithmic $z^2$ dependence of $\rho_4$.
- 地位：[C] — the form is suggested by the twist-4 OPE in perturbation theory (support as in `prop:lamet-support`), the hadronic structure is assumed.
- 文献：`Braun:2018brg`, `Radyushkin2017`, `JiEtAl2021`.
- 被使用于：`asm:lamet-large-z`, `thm:lamet-hadronic` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a twist-4 OPE for hadron matrix elements with control of the $z^2$ dependence (non-perturbative OPE).
- `\uses{prop:lamet-support, cor:lamet-entire}`.

#### `asm:lamet-uniform` — 扭度展开对 Ioffe 时间的一致性 [\tagC]
- **所属：** Ch22 `sec:lamet-power`. Technical.
- 陈述：(UI-a) coordinate form — the twist-2 remainder of the hadronic ITD is bounded by $K(-z^2)\Lambda^2(1+|\ln(-z^2\mu^2)|)^k$ uniformly in $\lambda$ for $0<-z^2\le z_0^2$; (UI-b) ray form — along $z^2=-\lambda^2/P_z^2$, $|\lambda|\le z_0P^z$, the remainder beyond twist 4 has Fourier transform $o(P_z^{-2})$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$.
- 地位：[C] — no all-order argument; evidence from the renormalon model (`prop:lamet-bvz`) and from solvable models; Liu's Gross–Neveu theorem covers only imaginary Ioffe time (`thm:yzliu-gn-convergence`, Ch23). The frontier report's hypothesis was reformulated (writer note in the Ch22 entry): a pointwise bound of type (UI-a) does not give a $P^z$-suppressed Fourier transform.
- 文献：`IzubuchiEtAl2018`, `Braun:2018brg`, `JiEtAl2021`.
- 被使用于：`thm:lamet-hadronic` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a non-perturbative OPE with remainder bounds uniform in $\lambda$ (cf. `asm:intro-np-ope`); as a first step a model proof at real Ioffe time.
- `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, prop:lamet-tmc}`.

#### `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair` — 静态色单态 $Q\bar Q$ 对的谱假设 [\tagC]
- **所属：** Ch23 `sec:yzliu-soft`. Technical.
- 陈述：(HP1) isolated colour-singlet ground state of the static pair at fixed $\vec b_\perp$ with residual energy $\gamma^{-1}V(\vec b_\perp)$, gapped continuum; (HP2) non-zero, boost-invariant overlap of the pair interpolating field; (HP3) Wilson-line self-energies and the pair time evolution cancel against $\sqrt{ZZ}$ (LSZ-type amputation; perturbatively `lem:yzliu-partial-exp`).
- 地位：[C] — non-perturbative spectral assumptions; (HP1) is supported by lattice static potentials (evidence).
- 文献：`Ji:2019sxk` (read), `Liu:2022nnk` §4.1–4.2, App. A.
- 被使用于：`cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：(HP1) from a gap in the static colour-singlet sector (confinement); (HP2), (HP3) from an LSZ-type construction for static sources in non-perturbative HQET.
- `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc}`.

#### `asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform` — 光锥极限对 ${\rm Im}\,\theta$ 的一致性 [\tagP]
- **所属：** Ch23 `sec:yzliu-rapidity`. Technical.
- 陈述：(UL) the light-cone limit $Y\to\infty$ of the single-time-ordered soft functions is uniform in ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in[-\pi,\pi]$.
- 地位：[P] — verified perturbatively for ${\rm Im}\,\theta\in\{0,\pm\frac\pi2,\pm\pi\}$ only.
- 文献：`Liu:2022nnk` §4.5–4.7, §6.
- 被使用于：`prop:yzliu-universality-classes` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a uniform bound on the $O(e^{-\theta})$ remainder in the strip at each order.
- `\uses{def:yzliu-hyperbolic-angle, thm:yzliu-analyticity}`.

#### `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` — 维数正规化微扰论中的 Wightman 结构 [\tagA]
- **所属：** Ch23 `sec:yzliu-analyticity`. Technical.
- 陈述：(WP1) order by order, in linear covariant gauges and DR at generic $\varepsilon\ne0$, gluon and ghost Wightman functions satisfy the spectral condition and local commutativity, so that their coordinate-space representations are boundary values of functions holomorphic in the (permuted extended) tubes, covariant under proper complex Lorentz transformations; (WP2) null separations on the same light-like line give scaleless contributions, so $\eta\to0^+$ may be taken at integrand level.
- 地位：[A]-level — formally a consequence of the covariant operator formalism (`KugoOjima1979`) and of the Bargmann–Hall–Wightman theorem (`Streater:1989vi`), used by Liu ("the author believes in it", `Liu:2023pjb` §4 item 2); not proven as a precise statement for DR at generic $\varepsilon$ (indefinite metric, gauge-dependent Green functions). It is the bottleneck of `thm:yzliu-analyticity`, `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`, `thm:yzliu-jet-continuation`.
- 文献：`Liu:2022nnk` App. F, `Liu:2023pjb` §2 and App. B, `Ji:2023pba` App. C.2, `KugoOjima1979`, `Streater:1989vi`.
- 被使用于：`thm:yzliu-analyticity`, `thm:yzliu-time-ordering`, `conj:yzliu-complex-wilson`, `thm:yzliu-jet-continuation` (direct `\uses` users in FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, computed at the merge).
- 解除途径：a proof that the DR parametric representations of all gluon/ghost Wightman functions with numerators are boundary values of holomorphic functions in the permuted extended tubes — e.g. by extending the positivity of $P_{ij}(\alpha)$ (`lem:yzliu-matrix-tree`) and the below-threshold argument of `thm:yzliu-time-ordering` to all graphs.
- `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst}`.



# PART 5 — VERIFIED_FORMULAS_v2 entries referenced (verbatim)

### VF-FRO-1 — Gordan 二择一与最小范数方向
For dual vectors $D_1,\dots,D_N$ on a finite-dimensional real space, exactly one of: (I) $\exists v$: $D_j\cdot v>0\ \forall j$; (II) $\exists\lambda_j\ge0$ not all zero with $\sum_j\lambda_jD_j=0$ (Landau point). If $0\notin K={\rm conv}\{D_j\}$, the min-norm point $w\in K$ satisfies $D_j\cdot w\ge|w|^2>0$.
- **Status:** V (OTHERS s1(a): 400 random instances, worst margin deficit $-10^{-14}$); classical (`Gordan1873`; the identification with Collins' geometric theorem: claim N02 confirmed, not found in print, elementary — a remark without novelty marker).
- **Used in:** Ch24 Landau section, `fig:front-gordan`; App D (pointer).

### VF-FRO-2 — Collins（2020，预印本）的物理区钳夹判据与符号条件
For $I(z)=\lim_{\epsilon\to0^+}\int_{\Gamma_0}d^dw\,B/\prod_j[A_j+i\epsilon]^{n_j}$ with $A_j$ at most quadratic and the sign condition: writing $A_j(w_S+\delta w)=D_j\cdot\delta w+\frac12\delta w\cdot E_j\cdot\delta w$, for every $v_0$ with $D_j\cdot v_0=0$ the non-zero $v_0\cdot E_j\cdot v_0$ of the relevant $j$ have one sign — the integration is trapped at $w_S$ iff the $D_j=\partial A_j(w_S)$ admit a Landau point. The sign condition holds for $k^2-m^2$ ($v_0\cdot E\cdot v_0=2\hat v^2\le0$), for eikonal (linear) denominators, non-relativistic propagators and approximated denominators quadratic in $k_T$ only.
- **Status:** V for where the sign condition holds (OTHERS s1(c)); theorem TR (`Collins:2020euz` read in full; arXiv only). Grade per FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 (D3: conservative; [R] only if re-derived in full).
- **Used in:** Ch24.

### VF-FRO-3 — 动量空间的 Coleman–Norton 方程（含无质量线与 Wilson 线）
Lines $k_j=\sum_L\eta_{jL}\ell_L+$(external), $\eta_{jL}\in\{0,\pm1\}$: trapped iff $\exists\lambda_j\ge0$ (not all zero, on the lines with $k_j^2=m_j^2$ resp. $n_j\cdot k_j=0$) with $\sum_j\eta_{jL}\lambda_j\Delta^\mu_j=0$ for every loop $L$, $\Delta_j=k_j$ (propagator) or $n_j$ (eikonal line); equivalently displacements $\Delta x_j=\lambda_j\Delta_j$ close around every loop (classical process).
- **Status:** V (OTHERS s1(b,d): collinear self-energy $(1-x,x)$, soft point, massive threshold, massive single on-shell line not trapped). Corollary (claim N03 confirmed, not new in substance: `Collins:2020euz` Thm 1 plus the Coleman–Norton closure); inherits the preprint status of VF-FRO-2.
- **Used in:** Ch24; `prop:ir-coleman-norton` (pointer).

### VF-FRO-4 — $\varphi^4_4$ 中 OPE 的收敛（Hollands–Kopper）
At fixed loop order $l$, massive Euclidean $g\varphi^4_4$, the remainder of the OPE summed over $[C]-[A]-[B]\le\Delta$ is bounded by $\ldots\times\frac1{\sqrt{\Delta!}}\big(\tilde Km|x|\sup(1,\frac{|\vec p|_n}m)^{n+2l+1}\big)^\Delta$ (full bound in OTHERS §2.5, their eq. (7)), so the OPE converges at every finite $|x|$.
- **Status:** TR (`Hollands:2011gf` read). [R] (model, fixed loop order).
- **Used in:** Ch24 OPE section, Ch21 (`thm:power-ope-convergence`).

### VF-FRO-5 — Jantzen 主恒等式（区域方法）
Under (1) disjoint covering domains $D_x$, (2) commuting expansions, (3) regularization of all integrals, (4) absolute convergence of each expansion on all of $D$ (+ exchange of summations):
$$\int_DI=\sum_xF^{(x)}-\sum_{\{x,x'\}}F^{(x,x')}+\sum_{\{x,x',x''\}}F^{(x,x',x'')}-\dots,\qquad F^{(x_1\cdots x_n)}=\int_DT^{(x_1)}\cdots T^{(x_n)}I;$$
overlap terms vanish when scaleless (the analogue of zero-bin subtractions).
- **Status:** TR (`Jantzen2011` §3 read). Identity [R](cond.); applicability [P].
- **Used in:** Ch24 regions section, Ch4 (`prop:ir-regions-minkowski`), Ch15 (zero-bin).

### VF-FRO-6 — Rothstein–Stewart 的 Glauber 拉氏量
$$\mathcal L_G^{\rm II(0)}=e^{-ix\cdot\mathcal P}\sum_{n,\bar n}\sum_{i,j=q,g}\mathcal O_n^{iB}\frac1{\mathcal P_\perp^2}\mathcal O_s^{BC}\frac1{\mathcal P_\perp^2}\mathcal O_{\bar n}^{jC}+e^{-ix\cdot\mathcal P}\sum_n\sum_{i,j=q,g}\mathcal O_n^{iB}\frac1{\mathcal P_\perp^2}\mathcal O_s^{j_nB},$$
$\mathcal O_n^{qB}=\bar\chi_nT^B\frac{\bar{\slashed n}}2\chi_n$, $\mathcal O_n^{gB}=\frac i2f^{BCD}\mathcal B^C_{n\perp\mu}\frac{\bar n}2\cdot(\mathcal P+\mathcal P^\dagger)\mathcal B^{D\mu}_{n\perp}$ (SCET conventions of Ch15).
- **Status:** TR (`RothsteinStewart2016` eq. (5.21) read). Construction [A].
- **Used in:** Ch15 (`rem:scet-glauber-dictionary`), Ch18, Ch24.

### VF-FRO-7 — 超领头对数的塔与闭式
$$\hat\sigma_n^{\rm SLL}=\Big(\frac{\alpha_s}{4\pi}\Big)^{n+3}L^{2n+3}\frac{(-4)^nn!}{(2n+3)!}\sum_{r=0}^n\frac{(2r)!}{4^r(r!)^2}C_{rn};\qquad \Delta\hat\sigma^{(S)}=-\hat\sigma_B\frac{4C_F}{3\pi}\alpha_s^3L^3\Delta Y\,{}_2F_2\Big(1,1;2,\frac52;-w\Big),\quad w=\frac{N_c\alpha_s}\pi L^2$$
($q\bar q$ singlet exchange, forward scattering); first SLL $\hat\sigma_1^{(S)}=\hat\sigma_B\frac4{15\pi^2}C_FN_c\alpha_s^4L^5\Delta Y$; ${}_2F_2(1,1;2,\frac52;-w)\simeq\frac32\frac{\ln w}w$ at large $w$ ($1.497$ at $w=10^8$).
- **Status:** V (OTHERS s4: term by term $n\le8$ from `Becher:2021zkk` eqs. (14), (19)). [A].
- **Used in:** Ch16 (`prop:jets-sll`), Ch24, `fig:front-sll`.

### VF-FRO-8 — 1-jettiness 中的相干性破坏对数（Banfi–Forshaw–Holguin）
$$\frac{d\sigma_1^{\rm CVL}}{dx_adx_bd\mathcal B}\simeq\sum_{a=q,g}\mathcal A_a\Big(\frac{\alpha_s}\pi\Big)^4(-i\pi)^2\frac1{480}\ln^6\frac1{\tau_1},\quad \mathcal A_q=\frac83N_c^2T_R^4f^q_Af^g_B\sigma^{(0)}_{qg},\quad \mathcal A_g=-\frac{32}3N_c^2T_R^4f^g_Af^q_B\sigma^{(0)}_{gq}$$
(PDFs at $\mu_F=\tau_1Q$; LL coherence-violation approximation).
- **Status:** TR (`Banfi:2025mra` read); v2.3: confirmed for the $qg\to q$ channel in SCET by `Becher:2026kbr` (two groups, different methods; claim E16). [V] for the standard exponentiation of $\tau_1$ at the level of this computation.
- **Used in:** Ch16, Ch24.

### VF-FRO-9 — $h\to\gamma\gamma$（$b$ 夸克圈）的端点因子化
Refactorization conditions $[\![\bar H_2(z)]\!]=-H_3J(zM_h^2)$, $[\![\langle O_2(z)\rangle]\!]=-\frac{g_\perp^{\mu\nu}}2\int_0^\infty\frac{d\ell_+}{\ell_+}J(-\bar n_1\cdot k_1\ell_+)S(\ell_+\ell_-)|_{z=\ell_-/\bar n_1\cdot k_1}$; renormalized theorem as in OTHERS §5.2 (`Liu:2020wbn` eq. (4.1)); leading double logarithms
$$\mathcal M_b\big|_{\rm LDL}=\mathcal M_0\hat L^2\sum_n\frac{n!}{(2n+2)!}\Big(-\frac{C_F\alpha_s}{2\pi}\hat L^2\Big)^n=\mathcal M_0\frac{\hat L^2}2\,{}_2F_2\Big(1,1;\frac32,2;-\frac{C_F\alpha_s}{8\pi}\hat L^2\Big).$$
- **Status:** series identity and its match to the NLO amplitude V (OTHERS s4); conditions and theorem TR (`Liu:2019oav`, `Liu:2020wbn` read). [A].
- **Used in:** Ch21 (NLP), Ch24, `fig:front-endpoint`.

### VF-FRO-10 — Yukawa 模型中单圈 $\overline{\rm MS}$ 分布可为负（Collins–Rogers–Sato）
$$f^{\rm renorm}_{q/p}(\xi;\mu)=a_\lambda(\mu)(1-\xi)\Big[\ln\frac{\mu^2}{\Delta(\xi)}-1+\frac{(m_q+\xi m_p)^2}{\Delta(\xi)}\Big]+O(a_\lambda^2),\quad \Delta(\xi)=\xi m_s^2+(1-\xi)m_q^2-\xi(1-\xi)m_p^2,\quad a_\lambda=\frac{\lambda^2}{16\pi^2};$$
for $m_q=0.3$, $m_p=1.0$, $m_s=1.5$ GeV: negative for $\xi>0.264$ at $\mu=0.8$ GeV (the OTHERS grid $0.02,\dots,0.98$ reports $[0.28,0.98]$), negative for $\xi>0.567$ at $\mu=1.2$ GeV, positive for all $\xi$ at $\mu=2.5$ GeV; $\xi\to1$ sign change at $\mu_*=m_s\exp\{\frac12[1-(m_q+m_p)^2/m_s^2]\}=1.70$ GeV; $\xi\to0$: bracket $\to\ln(\mu^2/m_q^2)$.
- **Status:** V (OTHERS s2: derivation of `CollinsRogersSato2022` eq. (55) from eq. (58), cut-off version; v2-check Q6: sign changes and $\mu_*$). [R] (model) — "MS-bar PDFs are positive at all scales" is [V].
- **Used in:** `conj:pdf-positivity` (Ch8, status paragraph), Ch24 positivity section, `fig:front-yukawa-pdf`.

### VF-FRO-11 — 由结构函数得到光子 PDF（LUXqed）
$$xf_{\gamma/p}(x,\mu^2)=\frac1{2\pi\alpha(\mu^2)}\int_x^1\frac{dz}z\Big\{\int_{\frac{x^2m_p^2}{1-z}}^{\frac{\mu^2}{1-z}}\frac{dQ^2}{Q^2}\alpha^2(Q^2)\Big[\Big(zp_{\gamma q}(z)+\frac{2x^2m_p^2}{Q^2}\Big)F_2\Big(\frac xz,Q^2\Big)-z^2F_L\Big(\frac xz,Q^2\Big)\Big]-\alpha^2(\mu^2)z^2F_2\Big(\frac xz,\mu^2\Big)\Big\},$$
$zp_{\gamma q}=1+(1-z)^2$, elastic parts $F_2^{\rm el}=\frac{G_E^2+\tau G_M^2}{1+\tau}\delta(1-x)$, $F_L^{\rm el}=\frac{G_E^2}\tau\delta(1-x)$, $\tau=Q^2/4m_p^2$. Point-like check: $xf_\gamma=\frac\alpha{2\pi}[1+(1-x)^2][\ln\frac{\mu^2}{m^2}-2\ln x-1]+\frac{\alpha m^2x^2(1-x)}{\pi\mu^2}$ (Frixione's $O(\alpha)$ result up to a power correction).
- **Status:** V (OTHERS s3); formula TR (`Manohar:2016nzj` eq. (6) read). [A].
- **Used in:** Ch11 (photon structure), Ch24.

### VF-FRO-12 — 领头阶 BK 方程
$$\frac{\partial S_Y(x,y)}{\partial Y}=\frac{\bar\alpha_s}{2\pi}\int d^2z\frac{(x-y)^2}{(x-z)^2(z-y)^2}\big[S_Y(x,z)S_Y(z,y)-S_Y(x,y)\big],\qquad \bar\alpha_s=\frac{\alpha_sN_c}\pi ;$$
the linearized kernel has eigenvalue $\bar\alpha_s\chi(\gamma)$ on $(x-y)^{2\gamma}$, $\chi(\gamma)=2\psi(1)-\psi(\gamma)-\psi(1-\gamma)$.
- **Status:** V (OTHERS s5; = VF-MISC-4). [A] within the eikonal (Regge) expansion.
- **Used in:** Ch17 (`thm:smallx-bk`), Ch24.

### VF-FRO-13 — 双部分子散射与重复计数的减除（Diehl–Gaunt–Schönwald）
$$\sigma=\sigma_{\rm SPS}+\sigma_{\rm DPS}-\sigma_{\rm sub},\qquad \frac{d\sigma_{\rm DPS}}{dx_1dx_2d\bar x_1d\bar x_2}=\sum\hat\sigma_{a_1b_1}\hat\sigma_{a_2b_2}\int d^2y\,\Phi^2(y\nu)F_{b_1b_2}(\bar x_i,y)F_{a_1a_2}(x_i,y),$$
$\Phi(u)\to1$ for $u\gg1$, $\Phi(u)=O(u^\delta)$ at small $u$, $\nu\sim Q$; $\sigma_{\rm sub}$ = the DPS formula with both DPDs replaced by their fixed-order splitting forms.
- **Status:** TR (`Diehl:2017kgu` §4.1 read). [A].
- **Used in:** Ch18 (`def:break-dpdf`), Ch24.

### VF-FRO-14 — 非全局可观测量的因子化（Becher–Neubert–Rothen–Shao）
$$\sigma(\beta,\delta)=\sum_{m\ge2}\big\langle\mathcal H_m(\{n\},Q,\delta)\otimes\mathcal S_m(\{n\},Q\beta,\delta)\big\rangle,\quad \mathcal S_m=\sum_{X_s}\langle0|S_1^\dagger\cdots S_m^\dagger|X_s\rangle\langle X_s|S_1\cdots S_m|0\rangle\,\theta(Q\beta-2E_{\rm out}),$$
$S_i(n_i)={\bf P}\exp(ig_s\int_0^\infty ds\,n_i\cdot A^a_s(sn_i){\bf T}_i^a)$ (their sign convention; convert $g\to-g$ for ours); RGEs $\frac d{d\ln\mu}\mathcal H_m=-\sum_{l\le m}\mathcal H_l\Gamma^H_{lm}$, $\frac d{d\ln\mu}\mathcal S_l=\sum_{m\ge l}\hat\Gamma^H_{lm}\otimes\mathcal S_m$.
- **Status:** TR (`Becher:2016mmh` §2 read). [A] ($e^+e^-$).
- **Used in:** Ch16 (`sec:jets-ngl`), Ch24.

### VF-FRO-15 — 双局域 PDF 算符的重整化：三角混合与矩约化
$$\begin{pmatrix}O\\A\\B\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}Z_{OO}&Z_{OA}&Z_{OB}\\0&Z_{AA}&Z_{AB}\\0&0&Z_{BB}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}O_{(0)}\\A_{(0)}\\B_{(0)}\end{pmatrix}\ \Rightarrow\ O=Z_{OO}O_{(0)}\ \text{in physical matrix elements};$$
moment reduction (OTHERS Prop. 8.1, hypotheses: local Joglekar–Lee/Henneaux classification for $\bar\psi\gamma^+(iD^+)^{N-1}\psi$, $F^{+\mu}(iD^+)^{N-2}F^+{}_\mu$; support in $[-1,1]$; generating-function identity): $\int_0^1dz\,z^{N-1}Z_{OO}(z)=Z^{(N)}_{\rm twist\text{-}2}$ for all $N$, $Z_{OO}$ fixed by its moments (Müntz–Szász for even/odd subsets).
- **Status:** triangular form TR (`Collins2011` eq. (11.20), pp. 407–412 read); Prop. 8.1 A (claim N04 confirmed, with prior art `Braun:2019qtp`; one-loop instance V in c_pdf §5.4). [A].
- **Used in:** `thm:prelim-composite-renorm` (remark), Ch8, Ch24 BRST section.

### VF-FRO-16 — 宽角振幅的红外森林公式（Ma 2020）
$$\Big[\sum_{F\in\mathcal F[A]}\prod_{\sigma\in F}(-t_\sigma)A\Big]_{\rm div}=0,$$
for massless wide-angle amplitudes with approximation operators $t_\sigma$ at the leading pinch surfaces (Feynman gauge); with the Ward identity this gives soft × jet × hard.
- **Status:** TR (`Ma2020` read). [A].
- **Used in:** Ch5 (pointer), Ch24.

### VF-FRO-17 — 小 $x$ 螺旋度演化：截距与 BER 的分歧（摘要层次）
Large-$N_c$ solution of the revised small-$x$ helicity evolution (Borden–Kovchegov): $\Delta\Sigma\sim\Delta G\sim g_1\sim(1/x)^{\alpha_h}$ with
$$\alpha_h\simeq3.66074\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_sN_c}{2\pi}}\quad(\text{BK}),\qquad \alpha_h^{\rm BER}\simeq3.66394\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_sN_c}{2\pi}}\quad(\text{infrared evolution equations});$$
the resummed $\Delta\gamma_{GG}(\omega)$ of BK agrees with the fixed-order anomalous dimensions through three loops and differs from BER from four loops on; the large-$N_c\&N_f$ analytic solution keeps a slight disagreement with BER and with Blümlein–Vogt.
- **Status:** T — transcribed from abstracts (OTHERS §9.5, abstract level); no computation by our agents. Grades: the BK results [A] (double-logarithmic approximation, large $N_c$ or large $N_c\&N_f$); the disagreement is an open problem, printed without a verdict (CLAIMS E34(iv), class Q3).
- **Literature:** `Kovchegov:2015pbl`, `Cougoulic:2022gbk`, `Borden:2023ugd`, `Adamiak:2023okq`, `Borden:2024bxa`, `Borden:2025ehe`; other side `Bartels:1995iu`, `Bartels:1996wc`, `Blumlein:1996hb` (identity fixed from the reference list of `Borden:2025ehe`).
- **Used in:** Ch24 small-$x$ section (Ch17 pointer).

### VF-LAM-0 — 记号
Ioffe time $\lambda\equiv P\cdot z=-P^zz$ for $z^\mu=z\hat e_z^\mu$; $a=\alpha_sC_F/(2\pi)$; $L_z=\ln(-z^2\mu^2e^{2\gamma_E}/4)$; $\ell_p=\ln(\mu^2/(4p_z^2))$, $p^z=|y|P^z$; $L_\zeta=\ln(\zeta_z/\mu^2)$, $\zeta_z=(2xP^z)^2$; plus distribution on a domain $D$: $\int_Dd\xi\,[g]^D_{+(1)}h=\int_Dd\xi\,g(\xi)[h(\xi)-h(1)]$. Dictionary: IJJSZ $\zeta=P^zz=-\lambda$, Radyushkin $\nu=-(p\cdot z)=-\lambda$, Ji et al. $\tilde\lambda=zP^z=-\lambda$.
- **Status:** V (LAMET §1.1).
- **Used in:** Ch22, Ch23.

### VF-LAM-8 — 动量空间单圈核（$\overline{\rm MS}$ 与比值方案）
$$C^{\overline{\rm MS}}(\xi)=\delta(1-\xi)+a\begin{cases}\big[\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\ln\frac\xi{\xi-1}+1+\frac3{2\xi}\big]^{[1,\infty]}_{+(1)}-\frac3{2\xi},&\xi>1\\[2pt]\big[\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\big(-\ell_p+\ln(\xi(1-\xi))\big)-\frac{\xi(1+\xi)}{1-\xi}\big]^{[0,1]}_{+(1)},&0<\xi<1\\[2pt]\big[-\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\ln\frac{-\xi}{1-\xi}-1+\frac3{2(1-\xi)}\big]^{[-\infty,0]}_{+(1)}-\frac3{2(1-\xi)},&\xi<0\end{cases}+a\,\delta(1-\xi)\Big(\frac32\ell_p+\frac52\Big),$$
$$C^{\rm ratio}(\xi)=\delta(1-\xi)+a\begin{cases}\big[\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\ln\frac\xi{\xi-1}+1-\frac3{2(1-\xi)}\big]_{+(1)},&\xi>1\\[2pt]\big[\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\big(-\ell_p+\ln(\xi(1-\xi))-1\big)+1+\frac3{2(1-\xi)}\big]_{+(1)},&0<\xi<1\\[2pt]\big[-\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\ln\frac{-\xi}{1-\xi}-1+\frac3{2(1-\xi)}\big]_{+(1)},&\xi<0\end{cases}$$
each plus distribution on its own domain; for $\gamma^z$ add $2a(1-\xi)\theta(0<\xi<1)$ ($\overline{\rm MS}$) resp. $a[2(1-\xi)]^{[0,1]}_{+(1)}$ (ratio); $\delta$ functions at $\xi=\pm\infty$ dropped (they do not contribute for $f(y)\sim y^{-1+a'}$, $a'>0$).
- **Status:** VL (LAMET s2: pairing with Gaussian test functions equals the Fourier transform of the coordinate kernels to $10^{-9}$–$10^{-12}$, both $\Gamma$; `IzubuchiEtAl2018` eqs. (quasi-matching), (quasi-matching-alt) read).
- **Used in:** Ch22 one-loop section.

### VF-LAM-14 — 准 PDF 匹配核的阈值因子化（单圈成分）
$$C\Big(\xi,\frac{p^z}\mu\Big)\Big|_{\xi\to1}=H\Big(\frac{4p_z^2}{\mu^2}\Big)\,p^z\,J_f\Big(\frac{(1-\xi)p^z}\mu,\frac{4p_z^2}{\mu^2}\Big),\qquad H^{(1)}=a\Big(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac{L_\zeta^2}2+L_\zeta\Big),$$
$$\tilde J^{(1)}=a\Big(\frac{L_z^2}2+L_z+\frac{\pi^2}{12}+2\Big),\qquad {\rm Im}\,J_f^{(1)}=a\pi\,{\rm sgn}(z)(L_\zeta-1),$$
$$\frac{d\ln H}{d\ln\mu}=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}L_\zeta+\tilde\gamma_H,\quad \frac{d\ln\tilde J}{d\ln\mu}=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}L_z-\tilde\gamma_J,\quad \tilde\gamma_J=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi+O(\alpha_s^2);$$
here $L_\zeta=\ln(4p_z^2/\mu^2)$ (written $L_z$ by Ji–Liu–Su) and $L_z$ is the coordinate log (their $l_z$). The one-loop summed anomalous dimension $\Gamma_{\rm cusp}[\ln(\lambda^2e^{2\gamma_E})-i\pi\,{\rm sgn}z]$ is $\mu$ independent.
- **Status:** V at one loop (LAMET s3: coordinate space to $|\lambda|=10^5$, momentum space, phase sign). The all-order factorization is [A]; NNLO consistency with 41 singular terms (two constants fitted) TR (`Ji:2023pba` read).
- **Used in:** Ch23 `thm:yzliu-threshold`, `ex:yzliu-threshold-oneloop` (owner), `fig:yzliu-threshold`; Ch22 `prop:lamet-oneloop`(e) (endpoint structure). (v2.2: "Used in" lines aligned with FINAL_OUTLINE_v2, merge decision MD9.)

### VF-LAM-15 — 准 TMD 因子化（Collins–Soper 指数含因子 $\frac12$）
$$\tilde f(x,b_\perp,\mu,\zeta_z)\sqrt{S_r(b_\perp,\mu)}=H\Big(\frac{\zeta_z}{\mu^2}\Big)\exp\Big[\frac12\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)\ln\frac{\zeta_z}\zeta\Big]f^{\rm TMD}(x,b_\perp,\mu,\zeta)\Big\{1+O\Big(\frac{\Lambda^2}{\zeta_z},\frac{M^2}{\zeta_z},\frac1{b_\perp^2\zeta_z}\Big)\Big\},$$
one loop ($a=\alpha_sC_F/(2\pi)$): $\tilde f^{(1)}=a[-\frac12L_b^2+L_b(\frac52-L_\zeta)-\frac32-\frac12L_\zeta^2+L_\zeta]$, $f^{{\rm TMD}(1)}=a[-\frac12L_b^2+(\frac32-\ln\frac\zeta{\mu^2})L_b+\frac12-\frac{\pi^2}{12}]$, $S_r^{(1)}=-2aL_b$, $\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$, hence $H^{(1)}=a(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac12L_\zeta^2+L_\zeta)$; momentum evolution $2\zeta_z\partial_{\zeta_z}\ln\tilde f=\tilde K+G$, $G^{(1)}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi(1-L_\zeta)$. The printed $e^{\tilde K\ln(\zeta_z/\zeta)}$ (`Ji:2019ewn` eq. (22), `JiEtAl2021`) leaves a residual $L_b(L_\zeta-\ln\frac\zeta{\mu^2})$ at one loop.
- **Status:** V (LAMET s4; YZLIU `check_qtmd_1loop.py`; independently re-checked by the spec-v2 drafter by hand). All-order statement [A]. Erratum confirmed: App Q row Q.11 (noted by the authors in PRD 105 (2022) 076014, footnote 1).
- **Used in:** Ch22 `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `ex:lamet-qtmd-oneloop`, `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` (owner, MD9); Ch23 `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`, `fig:yzliu-leading-region`; App Q.

### VF-LAM-16 — 由动量比值提取 Collins–Soper 核
$$\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)=\frac1{\ln(P_1^z/P_2^z)}\ln\frac{C(\mu,xP_2^z)\,\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,P_1^z)}{C(\mu,xP_1^z)\,\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,P_2^z)}+\text{power corrections},$$
equivalently $\frac{\tilde f(\zeta_{z,1})}{\tilde f(\zeta_{z,2})}=\frac{H_1}{H_2}\big(\frac{\zeta_{z,1}}{\zeta_{z,2}}\big)^{\tilde K/2}=\frac{H_1}{H_2}\big(\frac{P_1^z}{P_2^z}\big)^{\tilde K}$; $S_r$ and all $\Gamma$-, flavour- and spin-independent factors cancel; $x$ independence tests leading power.
- **Status:** V at one loop (LAMET s4: returns $\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$). [A] given VF-LAM-15.
- **Used in:** Ch22 `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`, `prop:lamet-tmd-ratios` (owner, MD9); Ch23 (reference).

### VF-LAM-18 — 类空关联函数的指数衰减（Ji–Liu–Su 2026，预印本）
Assumptions (spectrum with a gap $\Delta m$ above the external state; local tempered field with power bounds; locality). Then for the zero-momentum matrix element without one-particle intermediate states
$$f(0,\vec z)\equiv\langle\vec p=0|\phi(0,\vec z)\phi(0)|\vec p=0\rangle=2Z+O(e^{-\Delta m|\vec z|}),$$
with a sharp rate; QCD application (pion valence): $\tilde h^{\rm LA}=[A_2e^{i{\rm sgn}(z)\phi_2}+A_1e^{i{\rm sgn}(z)\phi_1}e^{-iP^zz}+A_3e^{i{\rm sgn}(z)\phi_3}e^{iP^zz}]e^{-\Lambda|z|}$, $\Lambda$ a heavy–light binding energy ($\approx0.5$ GeV).
- **Status:** TR (`Ji:2026vir`, arXiv only, read §§1–2, 5, 6). Theorem [R, R_cond] under the stated axioms; for QCD [A]/[C].
- **Used in:** Ch22 `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd` (owner); Ch23 `prop:yzliu-decay-extensions` (text only).

### VF-LAM-19 — 以强子动量代替部分子动量的 $O(\alpha_s)$ 差（早期匹配形式）
Replacing the parton momentum $|y|P^z$ by the hadron momentum $P^z$ in the one-loop $\overline{\rm MS}$ matching kernel changes it by
$$C\Big(\xi,\frac\mu{P^z}\Big)-C\Big(\xi,\frac\mu{|y|P^z}\Big)=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi\ln|y|\,\frac{1+\xi^2}{(1-\xi)_+}\,\theta(0<\xi<1)=-\frac{\alpha_s}\pi\ln|y|\Big[P_{qq}(\xi)-\frac32C_F\delta(1-\xi)\Big];$$
in the ratio scheme the difference is $-\frac{\alpha_s}\pi\ln|y|\,P_{qq}(\xi)$. The early range $\int_x^1$ also omits $|y|<|x|$ and $y<0$.
- **Status:** V (`verify/claims/batch_E01_s1.md` §7, `batch_E01_s2.md`; claim E06 confirmed; new in v2.3). The point is explicit in `IzubuchiEtAl2018` (text after eq. (27)); for quasi-GPDs `Ji:2015qla`.
- **Used in:** `rem:lamet-early-form`.

### VF-MISC-4 — BFKL 截距、BK 归一化与双对数极限
$$\omega_{\mathbb P}=\bar\alpha_s\chi(\tfrac12)=4\ln2\,\bar\alpha_s,\quad \chi(\gamma)=2\psi(1)-\psi(\gamma)-\psi(1-\gamma),\quad \bar\alpha_s=\frac{N_c\alpha_s}\pi;\qquad \partial_YN_{01}=\frac{\bar\alpha_s}{2\pi}\!\int\! d^2x_2\frac{x_{01}^2}{x_{02}^2x_{12}^2}\big[N_{02}+N_{12}-N_{01}-N_{02}N_{12}\big].$$
The linear part of the BK kernel has eigenvalue $\bar\alpha_s\chi(\gamma)$ on $(x_{01}^2)^\gamma$, so the BK normalization above and $\omega_{\mathbb P}=4\ln2\,\bar\alpha_s$ are consistent. Double-log limit: $\frac{\alpha_s}{2\pi}\gamma_{gg}(N)\to\frac{\bar\alpha_s}{N-1}$ as $N\to1$ (VF-PDF-8), hence at fixed coupling $xg\sim\exp\sqrt{4\bar\alpha_s\ln\frac1x\ln\frac{Q^2}{Q_0^2}}$ (saddle point of the inverse Mellin transform).
- **Status:** V (spec audit, `scratchpad/audit/checks1.py`, `checks3.py`: $\chi(\frac12)=4\ln2$; dipole-kernel eigenvalue $=\chi(\gamma)$ numerically at $\gamma=0.3,0.5,0.7$ to 8 digits; DLA saddle point analytic). The identification of this kernel with LL BK/BFKL is standard (`Balitsky1996`, `Kovchegov1999`), not re-derived.
- **Used in:** `thm:smallx-bfkl`, `thm:smallx-bk`, `ex:smallx-dla`.

### VF-YZL-5 — 准 TMD 因子化（与 VF-LAM-15 相同）与 CS 核比值
See VF-LAM-15; equivalently, in any on-light-cone regulator, $\tilde f=H\,\frac{f(x,b_\perp,\mu,\delta^-)}{S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta^+,\delta^-)}S(b_\perp,\mu,\delta^+,\hat z)$; $P^z\frac d{dP^z}\ln\tilde f=\tilde K+\mathcal G$, $\mathcal G^{(1)}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi(1-L_\zeta)$, $2\zeta_z\partial_{\zeta_z}\ln H=\mathcal G$; ratio $(\zeta_{z,1}/\zeta_{z,2})^{\tilde K/2}=(P_1^z/P_2^z)^{\tilde K}$.
- **Status:** V (YZLIU `check_qtmd_1loop.py`; LAMET s4). [A].
- **Used in:** Ch23 `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`; Ch22 `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` (the exponent; owner of the theorem, MD9).

### VF-YZL-6 — 硬核 $H$ 的单圈值与重—轻分解
$$H=1+\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{2\pi}\Big(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac{L_\zeta^2}2+L_\zeta\Big)=1+\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{4\pi}\Big(-L_\zeta^2+2L_\zeta-4+\frac{\pi^2}6\Big),\qquad H=C_{HL}^+C_{HL}^-,$$
$$C_{HL}^\pm=1+\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{8\pi}\Big(-4-\frac{5\pi^2}6+2L_\pm-L_\pm^2\Big),\quad L_\pm=L_\zeta\pm i\pi;\qquad \mu\frac d{d\mu}\ln H=\Gamma_{\rm cusp}L_\zeta+\gamma_C,\quad \gamma_C^{(1)}=-\frac{C_F}\pi\alpha_s .$$
The pair $(C_{HL}^\pm)^2$ is the LFWF kernel $H^\pm_{\rm LFWF}$; its one-loop phase is $\frac{\alpha_sC_F}2(1-L_\zeta)$ up to the ${\rm sgn}(z)$ convention.
- **Status:** V (YZLIU `check_HL_and_C1.py`; equal to the Ebert–Stewart–Zhao form). The product $C^+_{HL}C^-_{HL}$ was re-checked by hand by the spec-v2 drafter.
- **Used in:** Ch23.

### VF-YZL-12 — 时序无关性：末态求和恒等式与主等式
$$\int\frac{dE}{2\pi i}e^{-\nu|E|/\sqrt2}\Big[\prod_k\frac1{E-E_k-i\eta}-\prod_k\frac1{E-E_k+i\eta}\Big]=\sum_ke^{-\nu E_k/\sqrt2}\prod_{j\ne k}\frac1{E_k-E_j};$$
master equality (finite light-front lengths $L^\pm$, tilt $Y$, Z-factor subtractions, transverse links): $S^{\rm full}(L^+,L^-,b_\perp,\nu,Y,\varepsilon)=S_t^{\rm full}(-iL^-,-iL^+,b_\perp,\nu,Y,\varepsilon)$; invariant lengths below threshold, e.g. $x^2_{B,ij}=-\nu^2-b_\perp^2-\sqrt2i\nu(\lambda^L_i-\lambda^R_j)$ (real parts $\le-b_\perp^2-\nu^2<0$).
- **Status:** first identity V (YZLIU `check_HL_and_C1.py`, $n=2,3,4$); master equality TR (`Liu:2023pjb` read in full), [A] conditional on the perturbative Wightman structure of DR correlators.
- **Note (spec v2):** the proper complex Lorentz transformation relating $S_t$ and $S$ is $\Lambda(t,z)=(iz,it)$, the boost of rapidity $i\pi/2$ ($\cosh\frac{i\pi}2=0$, $\sinh\frac{i\pi}2=i$; determinant $+1$, preserves $t^2-z^2$), as in YZLIU §3.A6 and §6.3. YZLIU §3.A3 (summary of `Liu:2022nnk` App. F) writes $(it,iz)$: a slip — multiplication by $i$ reverses the sign of every interval and is not a Lorentz transformation. Checked by hand by the spec-v2 drafter.
- **Used in:** Ch23 time-ordering section, `fig:yzliu-complex-lorentz`.

### VF-YZL-17 — 欧氏直角尖点与 $\gamma_{HH}$ 的两圈差
In units of $(\alpha_s/\pi)^2$: $\Gamma^{(2)}_{\rm cusp}(\frac\pi2)=C_FC_A(\frac{\pi^2}{24}-\frac{49}{36})+\frac59C_FT_Fn_f$ and $\gamma^{(2)}_{HH}=C_FC_A(-\frac{49}{36}+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac{\zeta_3}2)+\frac59C_FT_Fn_f$, so
$$\Gamma^{(2)}_{\rm cusp}\big(\tfrac\pi2\big)-\gamma^{(2)}_{HH}=C_FC_A\Big(\frac{\zeta_3}2-\frac{\pi^2}{24}\Big)\approx0.190\,C_FC_A ;$$
at one loop both equal $-\alpha_sC_F/\pi$. The bent Euclidean soft functions (all cusps at Euclidean angle $\pi/2$) therefore give a wrong $\ln(\mu^2b^2)$ term at $O(\alpha_s^2)$.
- **Status:** V (`verify/claims/batch_E07_s1.md` §2.2, `batch_E07_s2.md`; claim E07(ii) confirmed; new in v2.3). The argument is that of `Ji:2019sxk`; the explicit value is our evaluation of the angle-dependent two-loop cusp anomalous dimension (`KorchemskyRadyushkin1987`).
- **Used in:** `thm:yzliu-soft-hqet` (status paragraph).

The time-ordering theorem is [A] conditional on DR analyticity; $N_m$ beyond large $\beta_0$ is [C]; do not print an exact loop order for the ultrasoft effect mentioned in `Liu:2022nnk`; dividing out staple self-interactions goes back to `Collins:2008ht` and `Ji:2018hvs`. The multiplicative-renormalizability proofs used are not Liu's. **A04 corrections (v2.3):** the APPB §4.4 remark that a detailed explanation beyond one loop "is not presented" concerns `Collins:2011ca` (APPB ref. [47]), not `CollinsSoper1981` (APPB App. D criticises the latter differently: a heuristic IR-safety argument with a loophole for linear divergences); `Ji:2018hvs` has five authors; cite the "open question" quote of `Vladimirov:2017ksc` from the JHEP version (p. 32). | `Liu:2022nnk`, `Ji:2019ewn`, `Vladimirov:2020ofp`, `Ebert:2022fmh`, `delRio:2023pse`, `Ji:2023pba`, `Collins:2008ht`, `Ji:2018hvs`, `Liu:2023onm`, `Liu:2021evw` |
| A05 | LaMET: exponential decay of space-like correlators is `Ji:2026vir` (preprint), building on `Fredenhagen:1984bz`; the matching theorem has two routes, `IzubuchiEtAl2018` (coordinate-space OPE) and `Ma:2014jla`/`MaQiu2018` (collinear factorization) — the RMP `JiEtAl2021` states that the proof is given only for perturbative free-quark states; `Ji:2014gla` asserted order-by-order provability without proof; renormalizability: `Ji:2017oey` (auxiliary field; the space-like extension is asserted), `Green:2017xeu`, `Ishikawa:2017faj` (degree counting, not full BPHZ); check the scope of `Braun:2020ymy` (three loops vs all orders) before quoting it; support: `Radyushkin:1983wh` (recalled — verify before attributing content); the threshold constants $c_a$, $c_H$ of `Ji:2023pba` were fitted; quote the "first" claim of `Avkhadiev:2024mgd` as theirs. | `Ji:2026vir`, `Fredenhagen:1984bz`, `IzubuchiEtAl2018`, `Ma:2014jla`, `MaQiu2018`, `JiEtAl2021`, `Ji:2014gla`, `Ji:2017oey`, `Green:2017xeu`, `Ishikawa:2017faj`, `Braun:2020ymy`, `Radyushkin:1983wh`, `Ji:2023pba`, `Avkhadiev:2024mgd` |
| A06 | Literature-absence statements only in the form 「据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索，尚未见……」 with the search basis, never as facts; each of the eleven items of CLAIMS A06 (Zimmermann-level OPE for space-like Wilson-line operators; Glauber analysis for quasi-TMDs; N³LO quasi-PDF kernel; refutation of the Rossi–Testa resolution; the announced RS SCET proof for DY; BBNS endpoint refactorization; twist-3 SSA factorization; flow-equation OPE for QCD with quarks; multilocal BRST classification; Gordan's theorem in the Landau literature; DGOPS: Glauber cancellation established only for DY) needs a repeated search at print time. Preprints are marked. **A06 corrections (v2.3):** item 3 is false — the N³LO non-singlet kernel exists (`Cheng:2024wyu`; used by `Ji:2024hit`); item 4 is false as stated — the Rossi–Testa resolution was contested in 2017–2018 with published exchanges on both sides (`Rossi:2018zkn`, `Rossi:2017muf`; replies `Karpie:2018zaz`, `Radyushkin:2018nbf`); the other items hold (item 8 not exhaustively; item 10 partially checked). | `RothsteinStewart2016`, `Diehl:2015bca`, `Collins:2020euz`, `Frob:2016mzv`, `Beneke:2026miy`, `Gamberg:2022lju`, `Bodwin:2025zzi` |

| A09 | OPE rigour: Zimmermann (scalar theory); `Hollands:2007zg` (pure Yang–Mills, gauge-invariant operators); `Frob:2016mzv` (preprint; bounds for pure YM only); convergence only in $\varphi^4_4$ at fixed loop order (`Hollands:2011gf`, `Holland:2014pna`); the theory class of `Anikin:1978tj` is unverified; the OTHERS grade "[R] in PT via classical results" is not adopted (§1.4). | `Hollands:2007zg`, `Frob:2016mzv`, `Hollands:2011gf`, `Holland:2014pna`, `Anikin:1978tj`, `IzubuchiEtAl2018` |

RS is supporting evidence, not a proof ("far from a proof of factorization, even in the abelian case"); RS §10.1–10.3 do treat the $n$–$\bar n$ production current at one and two loops (Glauber phases explicit in SCET$_{\rm II}$, equal to the soft zero-bin, or carried by ultrasoft graphs in SCET$_{\rm I}$) and expect the same at any order without proof — cite them for $e^+e^-$ only as one- and two-loop evidence (A11 item (3), corrected in v2.3); the BPS field redefinition does not remove Glauber couplings (RS §5.1); `Collins2011` §14.4.5 for "works equally well for the TMD case". | `AybatSterman2009`, `RothsteinStewart2016`, `Cheng:1969eh`, `Collins2011`, `CollinsSoperSterman1988` |
